# Confluid bug report — 2026-08-22 hunt

Third adversarial review of confluid (the first two: `BUGS-2026-08-13.md`, `BUGS-2026-08-19.md` — every entry of the
second is now marked FIXED / CLOSED / RULED / DEFERRED in its heading, and this hunt did not refile any of them).
**Every entry below was confirmed by running the reproduction shown** — the output is pasted verbatim (loggair banner
stripped). Run any example with `/Users/gert/taidal/.venv/bin/python script.py` from an empty directory (a few create
helper files first — the script says so).

**Method.** Seven independent line-by-line reviews, one per area (parse · scopes/refs · broadcast · engine ·
configure/dump/hydraide · introspection/validation · registry/surface), each checking the code against the contract in
`docs/lifecycle.md`, the area's `docs/*.md`, and `AGENTS.md`, and each bound by the scope ruling of 2026-08-20 (a
configuration utility, not a compiler: an ambiguous spelling that is REFUSED with a located error is correct, a wish
for per-key verdicts or position bookkeeping is not a bug, a required constructor argument is not a violation). Each
reviewer had to reproduce a defect before reporting it; two entries per area were then re-run independently from a
fresh directory (R3, R4, SR1, SR8, ENG-1, ENG-9, BC1, BC2, N2, N7, PA1, PA8, CD1, CD3 — see the status section) and all
reproduced byte-for-byte. Baseline at the start of the hunt (commit `243aa03`, clean tree): 1755 tests pass, all 25
`examples/*.py` run, mypy (workspace root) / flake8 / black / isort green — none of the bugs below is caught by the
existing gates.

**Glossary** — as before: *Target* = the recipe a `_target_:` mapping parses into; *PartialClass* = the deferred recipe
(`_partial_: true`); *marker* = any recipe object in a tree; *the load path* = `load()` passes 1–9; *the configure path*
= `configure(obj, config=…)` over live objects; *settled* = after pass 7 (kwargs final, nothing built).

Severity: **S1** = silent wrong answer on a mainstream spelling · **S2** = silent wrong answer on a plausible
spelling · **S3** = crash / untyped or unlocated error on legal input · **S4** = contract divergence with narrow
blast radius (two paths or two spellings disagree; docs ≠ code; a missing file:line).

IDs: `PA`=parse · `SR`=scopes/refs · `BC`=broadcast · `ENG`=engine · `CD`=configure/dump · `N`=introspection ·
`R`=registry. Where two reviewers hit the same root from different sides the surviving entry carries a
**Same root as:** line; exact duplicates were folded (PA7→SR1, SR8→PA4, PA6→SR4, BC2→CD1).
References to earlier reports' IDs are always qualified (`BUGS-2026-08-19 PA1`).

# P· parse time — loader / merger / interpolation / spelling / env (passes 1–3, 5–6)
## PA1 · S1 — The documented two-step door (`load(x, until="raw")` → `load(raw, scopes=…)`) loses the entry file's directory, so a scoped `include:` reads a file next to the CALLER (wrong file, silently) or raises not-found
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:1254` — `base_path = Path.cwd() / "string.yaml"` is the base for EVERY non-str/Path input; `:1276` `data = _import_and_include(data, base_path, {})` and `:1293` `data = _settle_scopes_and_includes(data, base_path, …)` then resolve the deferred (scope-block) include against the caller's CWD. The raw document carries no memory of the file it came from.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA1 — the documented two-step door loses the entry file's directory for a scoped include
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("sub").mkdir()
Path("sub/main.yaml").write_text("lr: 0.1\ntorch_only: !scope:framework=torch\n  include: overrides.yaml\n")
Path("sub/overrides.yaml").write_text("lr: 0.3\nsource: sub/overrides.yaml\n")
print("one-step :", load("sub/main.yaml", scopes=["framework=torch"], until="document"))
raw = load("sub/main.yaml", until="raw")                       # record 20's CLI idiom: raw first …
try:
    print("two-step :", load(raw, scopes=["framework=torch"], until="document"))   # … then activate
except Exception as e:
    print("two-step :", type(e).__name__, str(e).replace(str(Path.cwd()), "<cwd>")[:120], "...")
Path("overrides.yaml").write_text("lr: 0.9\nsource: CWD/overrides.yaml (WRONG FILE)\n")   # a decoy next to the caller
print("two-step+decoy:", load(load("sub/main.yaml", until="raw"), scopes=["framework=torch"], until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
one-step : {'lr': 0.3, 'source': 'sub/overrides.yaml'}
two-step : ConfigFileNotFoundError <cwd>/string.yaml includes overrides.yaml: Not found: overrides.yaml (searched: <cwd>/overrides.yaml, <cwd>/overrides.ya ...
two-step+decoy: {'lr': 0.9, 'source': 'CWD/overrides.yaml (WRONG FILE)'}
```
**Expected:** `{'lr': 0.3, 'source': 'sub/overrides.yaml'}` from both doors — `docs/architecture.md` record 20 shows exactly this idiom (`raw = load("experiment.yaml", until="raw")` … `document = load(raw, until="document", scopes=["framework=torch"])`) and says "passes already applied are idempotent"; `docs/search-paths.md`: tier 1 is "**The including file's directory** (include resolution only)". The error names `string.yaml`, a file that does not exist.
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-19 PA1 (same root — a deferred include loses its origin file); the two-step trigger and the decoy-reads-wrong-file outcome on the ENTRY file are NEW
## PA2 · S2 — An `include:` NESTED inside an inactive scope block's body (a sub-mapping, or a list-body item) IS opened: a missing overlay file aborts the load and a present one is read and listed
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:989-995` — the `ScopeBlock` branch of `_process_includes_recursive` walks the block's contents VALUE-wise (`data.contents = {k: _process_includes_recursive(v, …) for k, v in data.contents.items()}`) and, for a non-dict body, `data.contents = _process_includes_recursive(data.contents, …)`; only the block's own top-level `include:` key is deferred — every `include:` one level down hits the dict branch at `:1011-1012` before activation is known.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA2 — an include NESTED in an inactive scope block is opened (mapping body and list body)
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("main.yaml").write_text("lr: 0.1\ntorch_only: !scope:framework=torch\n  trainer:\n    include: torch_trainer.yaml\n")
try:
    print(load("main.yaml", until="document"))          # no activation: the block is dropped …
except Exception as e:
    print(type(e).__name__, str(e).replace(str(Path.cwd()), "<cwd>")[:90], "...")
Path("torch_trainer.yaml").write_text("x: 1\n")
doc, paths = load("main.yaml", until="document", return_paths=True)
print(doc, [p.name for p in paths])                    # … yet its file was read
Path("ops.yaml").write_text("ops:\n  - a\n  - - _scope_: {extra: }\n    - include: extra_ops.yaml\n  - b\n")
try:
    print(load("ops.yaml", until="document"))
except Exception as e:
    print(type(e).__name__, str(e).replace(str(Path.cwd()), "<cwd>")[:90], "...")
```
**Actual output:**
```
ConfigFileNotFoundError <cwd>/main.yaml includes torch_trainer.yaml: Not found: torch_trainer.yaml (searched: <cwd ...
{'lr': 0.1} ['main.yaml', 'torch_trainer.yaml']
ConfigFileNotFoundError <cwd>/ops.yaml includes extra_ops.yaml: Not found: extra_ops.yaml (searched: <cwd ...
```
**Expected:** `{'lr': 0.1}` with `['main.yaml']` in every case — `docs/interpolation.md`: "**An unactivated block never opens its file.** Scope resolution and include splicing alternate until they settle, so a block that is dropped takes its directive with it — a framework-specific overlay need not exist in a checkout that never activates that framework." Record 17: "A conditional include is genuinely conditional — the file is not read, not merely discarded."
**Known?** NEW (`tests/test_includes.py::test_include_inside_an_INACTIVE_scope_block_is_never_opened` pins the block's top-level key only)
## PA3 · S2 — `import:` written inside a marker's kwargs (nested or root marker) is neither consumed nor imported: it becomes a constructor kwarg named `import` and the module never loads
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:932` — `if isinstance(data, dict) and "import" in data:` is the ONLY pop; the `Fluid` arm at `:961-963` is `elif isinstance(data, Fluid): for value in data.kwargs.values(): _process_imports(value)` — it recurses into the kwarg VALUES but never treats `data.kwargs` itself as the mapping that may carry the key. (The include walk, `:981-983`, routes `Fluid.kwargs` through the dict branch for exactly this reason.)
**Repro:**
```python
# PA3 — `import:` inside a marker's kwargs is neither consumed nor imported
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load, configurable
Path("mymod.py").write_text("from confluid import configurable\n@configurable\nclass Thing:\n    def __init__(self, n=1): self.n = n\n    def __repr__(self): return f'Thing(n={self.n})'\n")
sys.path.insert(0, ".")
@configurable
class Host:
    def __init__(self, **kw): self.kw = kw
    def __repr__(self): return f"Host({self.kw})"
print("dict :", load("grp:\n  import: mymod\n  t: !class:Thing\n"), "| imported:", "mymod" in sys.modules)
sys.modules.pop("mymod")
print("marker:", load("h: !class:Host\n  import: mymod\n  t: !class:Thing\n"), "| imported:", "mymod" in sys.modules)
sys.modules.pop("mymod", None)
print("root  :", load("!class:Host\nimport: mymod\nt: !class:Thing\n"), "| imported:", "mymod" in sys.modules)
```
**Actual output:**
```
dict : {'grp': {'t': Thing(n=1)}} | imported: True
marker: {'h': Host({'import': 'mymod', 't': Thing(n=1)})} | imported: False
root  : Host({'import': 'mymod', 't': Thing(n=1)}) | imported: False
```
(`Thing` resolves here only because the dict case imported `mymod` earlier in the same process; in a fresh process the marker case fails with `UnknownClassError` and the `import` kwarg is the only trace.)
**Expected:** `Host({'t': Thing(n=1)})` and `imported: True` — `docs/lifecycle.md` pass 2: "`import: pkg.mod` — honoured in every mapping position, like `include:`"; AGENTS: "`loader._process_imports` walks dicts, lists and marker kwargs (a nested `import:` used to stay behind as a junk data key that could broadcast)".
**Known?** NEW (PA26's fix and pin `test_a_nested_import_is_consumed_like_an_include` cover a nested DICT only)
## PA4 · S2 — An overlay MAPPING at a slot holding a `!ref:` replaces the reference with a plain dict (identity and the referent's kwargs lost); the dotted spelling of the same override tunes the shared referent
**Where:** `confluid/merger.py:72` — `elif isinstance(existing, Target) and isinstance(value, dict):` is the tune arm; a `Reference` is not a `Target`, so it falls to `:143` `merged = _preserve_identity_copy(value)` (replace). The comment at `:92-94` keeps replace for a `Reference` "since the classifier calls those opaque" — written before the 2026-08-19 ruling that kwargs on a reference TUNE the referent (`resolver.fold_reference_kwargs`, which the dotted path reaches via `expand_dotted_mapping`'s `Fluid` arm at `:291-294`).
**Repro:**
```python
# PA4 — an overlay MAPPING at a `!ref:` slot replaces the reference; the dotted spelling tunes it
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class Box:
    def __init__(self, size=1, color=None): self.size, self.color = size, color
    def __repr__(self): return f"Box(size={self.size}, color={self.color})"
Path("base.yaml").write_text("proto: !class:Box {size: 3}\nuse: !ref:proto\n")
Path("block.yaml").write_text("include: base.yaml\nuse:\n  color: red\n")
Path("dotted.yaml").write_text("include: base.yaml\nuse.color: red\n")
for f in ("block.yaml", "dotted.yaml"):
    r = load(f)
    print(f, "->", r, "| use is proto:", r["use"] is r["proto"])
```
**Actual output:**
```
block.yaml -> {'proto': Box(size=3, color=None), 'use': {'color': 'red'}} | use is proto: False
dotted.yaml -> {'proto': Box(size=3, color=red), 'use': Box(size=3, color=red)} | use is proto: True
```
**Expected:** both files give `Box(size=3, color=red)` shared — `docs/interpolation.md`: "**An overlay mapping TUNES a marker, it does not replace it.** Re-stating a node your base file declared with `!class:` merges into that marker's kwargs, at any depth — the same thing the dotted spelling (`model.blue: 3`) does"; `docs/targets.md`: "Kwargs on a reference tune the one shared object … or a dotted path that walks through a reference". A `Reference` IS a marker (`Fluid`).
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-19 BC7 in symptom only (BC7 is the pass-7 merge sink; this is pass-3 composition through `deep_merge`) — NEW site
**Same root as:** SR8 (a mapping over a `Reference` replaces it in `deep_merge`) — folded into this entry.

## PA5 · S2 — An overlay marker whose target DIFFERS but shares the last dotted segment is "tuned": the overlay's target is silently discarded and the BASE class is built with the merged kwargs
**Where:** `confluid/merger.py:102-115` — `elif (isinstance(existing, Target) and type(value) is type(existing) and _same_marker_target(existing.target, value.target)): tuned = copy(existing); tuned.kwargs = deep_merge(existing.kwargs, value.kwargs)` — `copy(existing)` keeps `existing.target`; `_same_marker_target` (`:23`) compares `text.rsplit(".", 1)[-1]`, so `pkg_a.Trainer` and `pkg_b.Trainer` are "the same".
**Repro:**
```python
# PA5 — an overlay marker whose target differs but shares the LAST SEGMENT keeps the BASE target
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("base.yaml").write_text("t: !class:pkg_a.Trainer\n  lr: 0.1\n")
print(load("include: base.yaml\nt: !class:pkg_b.Trainer\n  epochs: 5\n", until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'t': Target(pkg_a.Trainer, {'lr': 0.1, 'epochs': 5})}
```
**Expected:** `Target(pkg_b.Trainer, …)` — the overlay wrote a different class. `docs/interpolation.md`: "Re-declaring the node instead — writing `!class:` again in the overlay — is a replacement, and the overlay's marker wins outright"; AGENTS: "There is ONE precedence rule: DOCUMENT ORDER, last spec wins". Even under the CD5 tune rule the later spec's TARGET must win; a same-name-different-module pair (`sonair.lightning.Trainer` / `sonair.keras.Trainer`) is the workspace's normal shape.
**Known?** NEW
## PA8 · S2 — The parsed-data door (`load(<dict>)`) skips every parse-time key-shape refusal: `a..b` mints a `''` key and `model._target_` becomes literal data, while the same text is refused
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:1262-1276` — the `else:` branch (`data = document_copy(data, copy_memo)` … `_import_and_include`) never runs `_refuse_malformed_keys` (which is called only from `map_constructor` `:799` and `_str_keyed_mapping` `:544`, i.e. from YAML nodes); `confluid/merger.py:286` `parts = key.split(".")` then expands whatever arrives.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA8 — the parsed-data door skips every parse-time key-shape refusal
from confluid import load
for text in ("a..b: 1", ".a: 1", "model._target_: Box"):
    try:
        print(f"text {text!r:24} ->", load(text, until="document"))
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"text {text!r:24} -> {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:50]} ...")
    key, _, value = text.partition(": ")
    print(f"dict {text!r:24} ->", load({key: value if key.endswith("_") else 1}, until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
text 'a..b: 1'                -> ConfigurationError: key 'a..b' at <unicode string>:1:1 has an EMPTY do ...
dict 'a..b: 1'                -> {'a': {'': {'b': 1}}}
text '.a: 1'                  -> ConfigurationError: key '.a' at <unicode string>:1:1 has an EMPTY dott ...
dict '.a: 1'                  -> {'': {'a': 1}}
text 'model._target_: Box'    -> ConfigurationError: 'model._target_' at <unicode string>:1:1 puts the  ...
dict 'model._target_: Box'    -> {'model': {'_target_': 'Box'}}
```
**Expected:** the same refusals from the dict door (a CLI building `{"model._target_": "X"}` overrides is the mainstream source of such dicts) — AGENTS: "every parse-time key-shape refusal goes through `loader._refuse_malformed_keys` … that is what keeps the two spellings from diverging"; record 20: "every stage takes every input shape … do not make a stage reachable for one input shape only"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "`model._target_: Box` could never become a marker — it used to leave a literal `_target_` key in the loaded config as ordinary data" (P16 — resurrected through the dict door).
**Known?** NEW (P16 / PA30 were fixed at the YAML-node gate only)
## PA9 · S2 — A one-line `.yaml` string containing a `:` (a Windows drive path) is parsed as YAML text and the path comes back as the "config"; an upper-case `.YAML` suffix does the same
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:1235-1238` — `if "\n" in data or ":" in data: return False` runs BEFORE `if data.endswith(_CONFIG_SUFFIXES): return True`, and `_CONFIG_SUFFIXES = (".yaml", ".yml")` (`:1218`) is case-sensitive.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA9 — a `.yaml` string with a `:` is parsed as YAML text, not treated as a file name
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
print(repr(load("C:\\configs\\experiment.yaml")))
print(repr(load("C:/configs/experiment.yaml")))
print(repr(load("X.YAML")))
try:
    load("configs/experiment.yaml")
except Exception as e:
    print(type(e).__name__)
```
**Actual output:**
```
'C:\\configs\\experiment.yaml'
'C:/configs/experiment.yaml'
'X.YAML'
ConfigFileNotFoundError
```
**Expected:** `ConfigFileNotFoundError` for all three — `docs/search-paths.md`: "(A `Path`, or a one-line string ending in `.yaml` / `.yml`, always names a FILE — a missing one raises rather than parsing as YAML text.)"; AGENTS: "a one-line `str` ending in `.yaml`/`.yml`, NAMES A FILE — at ANY length".
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-13 P6 B1 (`exp:v1.yaml`, still open) — the drive-letter face (docs ≠ code since PA17) and the suffix-case face are NEW
## PA10 · S3 — A UTF-16 config file (a BOM-marked YAML stream PyYAML itself reads) raises a raw `UnicodeDecodeError`
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:901-902` — `with open(path, "r") as f: data = yaml.load(f, Loader=ConfluidLoader) or {}` — a TEXT stream in the locale encoding; PyYAML's BOM/encoding detection only runs on a BYTES stream.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA10 — a UTF-16 config file (a BOM-marked YAML stream) raises a raw UnicodeDecodeError
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("u16.yaml").write_bytes("a: 1\n".encode("utf-16"))
import yaml; print("pyyaml from bytes:", yaml.safe_load(Path("u16.yaml").read_bytes()))
try:
    print(load("u16.yaml"))
except Exception as e:
    print(type(e).__name__, e)
```
**Actual output:**
```
pyyaml from bytes: {'a': 1}
UnicodeDecodeError 'utf-8' codec can't decode byte 0xff in position 0: invalid start byte
```
**Expected:** `{'a': 1}` (open in binary and let PyYAML detect the BOM), or a located `ConfigurationError` naming the file — AGENTS: "Every error confluid raises MUST come from `confluid/exceptions.py` (root `ConfluidError`), never a bare builtin". (The 2026-08-19 suspected list noted the encoding risk as non-reproducible on macOS; a UTF-16 file — what Windows PowerShell `>` writes — reproduces it on any platform.)
**Known?** NEW
## PA11 · S3 — The legacy colon-free `!class` / `!ref` tags with a mapping body raise a raw PyYAML `ConstructorError`
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:744-748` — `ref_compat` / `class_compat` call `loader.construct_scalar(node)` unconditionally; a `MappingNode` makes PyYAML raise. The `!class:` / `!ref:` constructors one screen up (`:601-633`) refuse the same shape mismatches with a located `ConfigurationError`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA11 — the legacy colon-free `!class` / `!ref` tags with a mapping body raise raw PyYAML errors
from confluid import load
for text in ("m: !class\n  a: 1\n", "m: !ref\n  a: 1\n"):
    try:
        print(load(text, until="document"))
    except Exception as e:
        print(type(e).__name__, str(e).splitlines()[0])
```
**Actual output:**
```
ConstructorError expected a scalar node, but found mapping
ConstructorError expected a scalar node, but found mapping
```
**Expected:** a located `ConfigurationError` (or drop the legacy registration) — AGENTS: "Every error confluid raises MUST come from `confluid/exceptions.py` … never a bare builtin"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "A marker that does not make sense is a located `ConfigurationError` at load, never a silently degraded value — in BOTH spellings."
**Known?** NEW
## PA12 · S4 — An unreadable config file raises a raw `PermissionError`; reached through `include:` the including file is not named
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:901` — `with open(path, "r") as f:` is unguarded; the `is_dir()` / `exists()` checks at `:887-899` cover only two of the `OSError` family and they are the only ones that render `"{including} includes {requested}: "`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA12 — an unreadable config file raises a raw PermissionError; via include: the including file is not named
import os
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("secret.yaml").write_text("a: 1\n"); os.chmod("secret.yaml", 0)
Path("main.yaml").write_text("include: secret.yaml\nb: 2\n")
for f in ("secret.yaml", "main.yaml"):
    try:
        print(load(f))
    except Exception as e:
        print(f, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e).replace(str(Path.cwd()), "<cwd>"))
os.chmod("secret.yaml", 0o644)
```
**Actual output:**
```
secret.yaml -> PermissionError [Errno 13] Permission denied: '<cwd>/secret.yaml'
main.yaml -> PermissionError [Errno 13] Permission denied: '<cwd>/secret.yaml'
```
**Expected:** a `ConfluidError` naming the file and, for an include, `main.yaml includes secret.yaml: …` — AGENTS: "never a bare builtin" and (PA25 fix) "an include miss names the INCLUDING file".
**Known?** NEW
## PA13 · S4 — A `$$` inside a `${...}` placeholder destroys the placeholder: `${ROOT:a$$b}` with `ROOT` SET stays literal text
**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:748-752` — `if "$$" in value: return "$".join(self._interpolate_embedded(part, …) for part in value.split("$$"))` splits the string BEFORE any placeholder is matched, so `${ROOT:a` and `b}` are interpolated as two fragments that match nothing.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA13 — a `$$` inside a `${...}` placeholder destroys the placeholder
import os
from confluid import load
os.environ["ROOT"] = "/store"
print(load("a: ${ROOT:a$$b}\nb: ${env:ROOT,a$$b}\nc: ${ROOT:ab}\n", until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'a': '${ROOT:a$b}', 'b': '${env:ROOT,a$b}', 'c': '/store'}
```
**Expected:** `{'a': '/store', 'b': '/store', 'c': '/store'}` (the variable is set; the default is never needed) — `docs/interpolation.md`: "**`$$` is a literal `$`.** The escape suppresses every interpolation spelling" and "`${PORT:8080}` (with an optional `:default`)". A default containing a literal `$` (`${PRICE:$$5}`) has no working spelling.
**Known?** NEW
## PA14 · S4 — The `$$` escape is not idempotent across the document stage: `load(load(x, until="document")) != load(x)` for any `$$NAME`
**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:752` collapses `$$` to `$` at pass 6; the second `load()` re-runs pass 6 on the already-collapsed text and `:754` / `:807-808` (`_expand_bare_env`) expands the now-bare `$NAME`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA14 — the `$$` escape is not idempotent across the document stage
import os
from confluid import load
os.environ["RUN_USER"] = "gert"
text = "command: echo $$RUN_USER\n"
once = load(text, until="document")
print("once :", once)
print("twice:", load(once, until="document"))
print("load(load(x, until='document')) == load(x):", load(once) == load(text))
```
**Actual output:**
```
once : {'command': 'echo $RUN_USER'}
twice: {'command': 'echo gert'}
load(load(x, until='document')) == load(x): False
```
**Expected:** `echo $RUN_USER` both times — `docs/lifecycle.md`: "Passes already applied to the data are idempotent, so `load(load(x, until="document"))` is `load(x)`." This is the round-trip escape `dump()` emits (CD10), so every `load(until="document")` → CLI merge → `load(document)` pipeline re-expands a dumped literal `$`.
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-19 PA18 (same idempotence breach, env-value trigger); the `$$`-escape trigger postdates it (2026-08-20) — NEW face
## PA15 · S4 — Bare `$VAR` and `${VAR}` disagree whenever the env value is YAML-coercible: `${V}/x` yields `8/x`, `90/x`, `True/x`, or stays a LITERAL for a date, while `$V/x` substitutes the text
**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:754` → `_expand_bare_env` (`:807-808`, text only) vs `:860` / `:897` `return self._parse_primitive(env_val), True` (YAML coercion) followed by `:782-784` `if found and _is_scalar(resolved): return str(resolved)` / `return match.group(0)` (a `date` is not `_is_scalar`, so the placeholder is left in place).
**Repro:**
```python
# PA15 — bare `$VAR` and `${VAR}` disagree whenever the env value is YAML-coercible
import os
from confluid import load
for value in ("010", "1:30", "2026-08-14", "yes"):
    os.environ["V"] = value
    print(f"V={value!r:13}", "bare:", load("p: $V/x", until="document")["p"], "| braced:", load("p: ${V}/x", until="document")["p"])
```
**Actual output:**
```
V='010'         bare: 010/x | braced: 8/x
V='1:30'        bare: 1:30/x | braced: 90/x
V='2026-08-14'  bare: 2026-08-14/x | braced: ${V}/x
V='yes'         bare: yes/x | braced: True/x
```
**Expected:** the same text in both columns — `docs/interpolation.md`: "so `root: $DATA_ROOT/subdir` behaves exactly like `root: ${DATA_ROOT}/subdir` on every entry path". An embedded substitution is text; coercing `010` to `8` inside a path is a silent wrong answer.
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-13 P14 (the coercion itself); the documented two-spellings equivalence being false is NEW
## PA16 · S4 — `$$` collapses in plain mapping keys but NOT in a marker's kwarg keys, so a `**kwargs` class with a `$` in a key breaks `load(emit(x)) == load(x)`
**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:582` — the dict branch collapses keys (`k.replace("$$", "$") if isinstance(k, str) and "$$" in k else k`); `:616-617` `_interpolate_fluid_kwargs` does `for key in list(kwargs): kwargs[key] = self._interpolate_only(kwargs[key], …)` — values only. `dump()` escapes every key uniformly, so the emitted `a$$b` reloads as `a$$b`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA16 — `$$` collapses in plain mapping keys but not in a marker's kwarg keys; the emit round trip breaks
from confluid import load, configurable
from confluid.hydraide import emit
@configurable
class KW:
    def __init__(self, **kw): self.kw = kw
    def __repr__(self): return f"KW({self.kw})"
print("plain dict key:", load("d:\n  a$$b: 1\n", until="document"))
src = "k: !class:KW\n  a$b: 1\n"
print("load(src)      :", load(src))
print("emit(src)      :", repr(emit(src)))
print("load(emit(src)):", load(emit(src)))
```
**Actual output:**
```
plain dict key: {'d': {'a$b': 1}}
load(src)      : {'k': KW({'a$b': 1})}
emit(src)      : 'k:\n  _target_: KW\n  a$$b: 1\n'
load(emit(src)): {'k': KW({'a$$b': 1})}
```
**Expected:** `KW({'a$b': 1})` after the round trip — `docs/interpolation.md`: "It collapses in mapping **keys** too — this is the escape `dump()` emits"; AGENTS: "`load(emit(x)) == load(x)` is the pinned contract"; "the escape is uniform over keys and values".
**Known?** NEW
## PA17 · S4 — Duplicate-key identity is `(tag, TEXT)`, not `(tag, value)`: `{1: a, 01: b}`, `{yes: a, true: b}`, `{~: a, null: b}`, `{0x10: a, 16: b}` collapse silently to the last value
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:514` — `identity = (key.tag, key.value)` — `ScalarNode.value` is the raw scalar TEXT, so two spellings of one constructed key are two identities and PyYAML's last-wins then runs unrefused.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA17 — duplicate-key identity is (tag, TEXT): the same key spelled two ways collapses silently
from confluid import load
for doc in ("{1: a, 01: b}", "{yes: a, true: b}", "{~: a, null: b}", "{0x10: a, 16: b}", "{a: 1, 'a': 2}"):
    try:
        print(f"{doc:20} ->", load(doc, until="document"))
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"{doc:20} -> {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:40]} ...")
```
**Actual output:**
```
{1: a, 01: b}        -> {1: 'b'}
{yes: a, true: b}    -> {True: 'b'}
{~: a, null: b}      -> {None: 'b'}
{0x10: a, 16: b}     -> {16: 'b'}
{a: 1, 'a': 2}       -> ConfigurationError: duplicate key 'a' at <unicode string>:1: ...
```
**Expected:** the first four refused like the fifth — AGENTS / `_refuse_duplicate_keys` docstring: "identity is `(tag, value)`, never text — `1:` and `"1":` are an int key and a str key and PyYAML keeps both"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "**A duplicate key is refused.** … Confluid raises instead, because the discarded value is invisible."
**Known?** NEW
## PA18 · S4 — A reserved key in a tag's INLINE kwargs (`!class:Foo(_target_=Bar)`, `(sub._target_=Bar)`) is accepted as a constructor kwarg; the block-body spelling is refused
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:667-668` — `if isinstance(node, yaml.nodes.ScalarNode) and instant: return _stamp(_make_fluid(factory, name, inline), loader, node)` — `_refuse_reserved_keys_in_tag_body` (`:660`) and the dotted-reserved gate run only on the `MappingNode` path at `:658-665`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA18 — a reserved key in the INLINE kwargs of a tag is accepted; in the block body it is refused
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class Foo:
    def __init__(self, **kw): self.kw = kw
    def __repr__(self): return f"Foo({self.kw})"
print("inline:", load("m: !class:Foo(_target_=Bar)"))
print("inline:", load("m: !class:Foo(sub._target_=Bar)", until="document"))
try:
    load("m: !class:Foo\n  _target_: Bar\n")
except Exception as e:
    print("block :", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:70], "...")
```
**Actual output:**
```
inline: {'m': Foo({'_target_': 'Bar'})}
inline: {'m': Target(Foo, {'sub._target_': 'Bar'})}
block : ConfigurationError !class:Foo at <unicode string>:1:4 carries the reserved key(s) ['_targ ...
```
**Expected:** the inline spellings refused with the block spelling's message — `docs/plain-format.md` lists "`x: !class:Foo` / `_target_: Bar` — a reserved key inside a tagged body — one spelling too many" among the refusals; AGENTS: "`_refuse_reserved_keys_in_tag_body` refuses a reserved key inside a `!class:`/`!partial:` body".
**Known?** NEW (PA28 fixed the block body only)
## PA19 · S4 — A reserved key at the top level of a `!scope:` TAG body splices into the parent as literal data (`_target_: Foo`, `_ref_: x`), while the `_scope_:` spelling of the same document is refused
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:722` — `contents = _str_keyed_mapping(loader, node)` builds the scope body with no reserved-key check (the exemption comment at `:639-641`: "Class-family tags only: a scope block's body keys are ordinary config keys and may legitimately splice a `_target_:` mapping" — a NESTED mapping, not a top-level key); the reserved-key path (`:250-252`) refuses `_scope_` beside `_target_` as "Conflicting reserved keys".
**Repro:**
```python
# PA19 — a reserved key at the top level of a `!scope:` TAG body splices as literal data; the `_scope_` spelling refuses
from confluid import load
tag = "blk: !scope:debug\n  _target_: Foo\n  a: 1\n"
rk  = "blk:\n  _scope_: {debug: }\n  _target_: Foo\n  a: 1\n"
for label, text in (("tag", tag), ("rk ", rk)):
    try:
        print(label, "->", load(text, scopes=["debug"], until="document"))
    except Exception as e:
        print(label, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:75])
print("tag", "->", load("x: !class:collections.Counter\nblk: !scope:debug\n  _ref_: x\n", scopes=["debug"], until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
tag -> {'_target_': 'Foo', 'a': 1}
rk  -> ConfigurationError Conflicting reserved keys in one mapping: _scope_, _target_ (at <unicode st
tag -> {'x': Target(collections.Counter, {}), '_ref_': 'x'}
```
**Expected:** the tag spelling refused like the reserved-key one — AGENTS: "Both MUST produce the SAME Fluid markers. A behaviour reachable from only one spelling is a BUG in that spelling"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "A marker that does not make sense is a located `ConfigurationError` at load, never a silently degraded value — in BOTH spellings."
**Known?** NEW
## PA20 · S4 — FIXED 2026-08-22 (located in both spellings — `tests/test_scopes.py`) — The removed scope call form (`!scope:task(cls)`, `default_scopes: [task(cls)]`) raises a `ScopeError` with no `file:line:col`, although the YAML node (tag) or the file (`default_scopes`) is in hand
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:711` — `key, value = _parse_scope_suffix(tag_suffix)` runs before `_node_where(node, loader)` is ever computed (`:713-719`); `confluid/scopes.py:91` — `key, val = parse_scope_arg(raw)` inside `parse_default_scopes(value, where=…)` lets `:58` `raise ScopeError(f"scope activation {stripped!r}: the call form was removed …")` escape without the `where` prefix the shape errors get.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA20 — the removed call form raises a ScopeError with NO location although the node / file is in hand
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("c.yaml").write_text("x: 1\nblk: !scope:task(cls)\n  a: 1\n")
Path("d.yaml").write_text("default_scopes: [task(cls)]\nblk: !scope:task=cls\n  a: 1\n")
for f in ("c.yaml", "d.yaml"):
    try:
        load(f)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f, "->", type(e).__name__, e)
```
**Actual output:**
```
c.yaml -> ScopeError scope activation 'task(cls)': the call form was removed — write 'task=cls'
d.yaml -> ScopeError scope activation 'task(cls)': the call form was removed — write 'task=cls'
```
**Expected:** `… at c.yaml:2:6` / `c.yaml: default_scopes …` — AGENTS: "EVERY error raised while processing a document NAMES ITS `file:line:col`"; "a malformed `default_scopes:` leads with its file (`parse_default_scopes(where=…)`)".
**Known?** NEW (SR16 removed the form; its refusal is unlocated)
## PA21 · S4 — A bracket-indexed dotted WRITE (`a[0].b: 1`) mints a literal key `a[0]` that the READ grammar (`${a[0].b}`) cannot reach; the `a.0` spelling of the same write is refused
**Where:** `confluid/merger.py:286` — `parts = key.split(".")` — the write grammar knows only `.`; `confluid/resolver.py:172-208` `_parse_path_segments` reads `[0]` as an index. PA9 (2026-08-19) added the list refusal for the `a.0` spelling only (`:299-306`).
**Repro:**
```python
# PA21 — a bracket-indexed dotted WRITE mints a literal `a[0]` key the read grammar cannot reach
from confluid import load
print(load("a: [{b: 0}]\na[0].b: 1\n", until="document"))
print(load("a: [{b: 0}]\nx: ${a[0].b}\n", until="document"))
print(load("a[0].b: 1\nx: ${a[0].b}\n", until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'a': [{'b': 0}], 'a[0]': {'b': 1}}
{'a': [{'b': 0}], 'x': 0}
{'a[0]': {'b': 1}, 'x': '${a[0].b}'}
```
**Expected:** the same refusal `a.0: 99` gets — `docs/lifecycle.md` pass 5: "a dotted write INTO a list (`a.0: 99` — the read grammar indexes a list, a write cannot)" is refused; `docs/interpolation.md`: "Dotted / bracketed name → another config key … `${items[0]}`" — one grammar, and a key the engine writes must be one it can read.
**Known?** NEW (sibling of BUGS-2026-08-19 PA9, fixed for `a.0`)
## PA22 · S4 — A dotted key written INSIDE a block (`train: {opt.lr: 0.1}`) is unreachable by `${train.opt.lr}` (silent literal); the identical top-level `train.opt.lr: 0.1` resolves
**Where:** `confluid/merger.py:355-359` (docstring) — "Only the document's TOP-LEVEL keys are expanded; dotted keys inside nested blocks are expanded lazily at block-consumption time by `broadcast._expand_block_keys`" — so at pass 6 the tree holds a literal `'opt.lr'` key; `confluid/resolver.py:700-704` `_lookup_path` walks `_parse_path_segments("train.opt.lr")` = `train` → `opt` → miss, and `:898-900` leaves the literal.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA22 — a dotted key written INSIDE a block is not reachable by `${...}`; the same key at the top level is
from confluid import load
print(load("train:\n  opt.lr: 0.1\nx: ${train.opt.lr}\n", until="document"))
print(load("train.opt.lr: 0.1\nx: ${train.opt.lr}\n", until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'train': {'opt.lr': 0.1}, 'x': '${train.opt.lr}'}
{'train': {'opt': {'lr': 0.1}}, 'x': 0.1}
```
**Expected:** `0.1` in both (or a refusal naming the nested dotted key) — `docs/interpolation.md`: "a `${a.b}` reads the EXPANDED tree — the same answer the returned document gives"; the nested dotted spelling IS the class-block form (`Trainer: {optimizer.lr: 0.1}`), and no `${...}` spelling can read it (`${train[opt.lr]}` misses too: the `idxref` resolves `opt.lr` against the root).
**Known?** NEW (PA8 fixed top-level expansion order only)
## PA23 · S4 — `${ref:}` with an EMPTY path survives as literal text at every stage; `${ref: }` is refused
**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:55` — `_INTERP_RE = re.compile(r"\$\{([\w.\[\]-]+)(?::([^}]+))?\}")`: `[^}]+` needs one character after the colon, so `${ref:}` is not a placeholder at all; `:429-430` (`hoist_marker_placeholders`) and `:757` (`_interpolate`) never see it, and the `needs a target path` refusal at `:835-836` fires only for `${ref: }`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA23 — `${ref:}` with an empty path survives as literal text; `${ref: }` is refused
from confluid import load
print(load("x: ${ref:}\n", until="document"))
print(load("x: ${ref:}\n"))
try:
    load("x: '${ref: }'\n", until="document")
except Exception as e:
    print(type(e).__name__, e)
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'x': '${ref:}'}
{'x': '${ref:}'}
ConfigurationError ${ref:...} needs a target path
```
**Expected:** the same `needs a target path` refusal — `docs/plain-format.md`: "A marker that does not make sense is a located `ConfigurationError` at load, never a silently degraded value"; a `${ref:` prefix is the reference spelling and cannot be an ordinary string.
**Known?** NEW
## PA24 · S4 — `${env:PORT:8080}` (the `:default` form the docs give for `${PORT:8080}`) is left literal whether or not `PORT` is set; `${PORT:8080}` and `${env:PORT,8080}` both resolve
**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:855-858` — `if name in _ENV_RESOLVERS: arg = default_val or ""; var, _, fallback = arg.partition(","); env_val = os.getenv(var.strip())` — the regex hands `PORT:8080` as the default group, the env resolver reads the variable `PORT:8080` (never set) and, with no comma, has no fallback, so `:900` returns the literal.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA24 — `${env:NAME:default}` (the `:default` spelling the docs give for `${NAME:default}`) is left literal

from confluid import load
os.environ.pop("PORT", None)
print(load("a: ${PORT:8080}\nb: ${env:PORT,8080}\nc: ${env:PORT:8080}\n", until="document"))
os.environ["PORT"] = "9000"
print(load("a: ${PORT:8080}\nb: ${env:PORT,8080}\nc: ${env:PORT:8080}\n", until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'a': 8080, 'b': 8080, 'c': '${env:PORT:8080}'}
{'a': 9000, 'b': 9000, 'c': '${env:PORT:8080}'}
```
**Expected:** `c` resolves like `a`/`b`, or is refused — not a silent literal that also ignores a SET variable. `docs/interpolation.md`: "`${PORT:8080}` (with an optional `:default`)"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "`port: ${env:PORT,8080}  # comma default`" — two default grammars on one placeholder family, and mixing them degrades silently.
**Known?** NEW

## PA26 · S4 — The list-form scope gate reads literal key names only: a `<<:`-merged `_scope_` first item does not guard the list — the guarded item leaks as a nested list with no activation
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:837-839` — `marks_a_scope = isinstance(first, yaml.nodes.MappingNode) and any(isinstance(k, yaml.nodes.ScalarNode) and k.value in (SCOPE_KEY, NOTSCOPE_KEY) for k, _ in first.value)` — a raw scan of `first.value`, not `_node_key_names(first)`, so a merge key hides the marker; the inner list then stays a plain list whose first element is a `ScopeBlock` with an empty body.

```
no activation : {'ops': ['a', ['guarded'], 'b']}
literal first : {'ops': ['a', 'b']}
```
**Expected:** `['a', 'b']` for both — AGENTS: "the key-name read SEES THROUGH merge keys, and that is `loader._node_key_names` … Never simplify the helper back to a comprehension over `node.value`"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "Anchors and merge keys work on markers".

## PA27 · S4 — `to_tags` leaves three convertible reserved-key shapes untouched WITHOUT a `Finding`, so `convert_file` answers "not written, no findings"
**Where:** `confluid/spelling.py:373-374` — `if not dash_first_key and value_wo_anchor: continue  # a scalar-valued key — nothing to convert` swallows a list item whose first key is not reserved (`- name: a` / `_target_: X` below it); `:168-169` `if not (stripped.startswith("{") and stripped.endswith("}")): return None` swallows a flow mapping spanning lines; `_KEY_LINE` (`:44`, `[^:#]*?`) cuts a quoted key at its first `:` and `:373-374` swallows the rest. None of the three appends a `Finding`.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA27 — `to_tags` leaves convertible reserved-key sites untouched WITHOUT a Finding
from pathlib import Path
from confluid.spelling import to_tags, convert_file
for label, text in (("item, _target_ not first", "items:\n  - name: a\n    _target_: collections.Counter\n    size: 2\n"),
                    ("multi-line flow        ", "m: {_target_: collections.Counter,\n    a: 2}\n"),
                    ("quoted key with colon  ", "\"a:b\": {_target_: collections.Counter, a: 2}\n")):
    out, findings = to_tags(text)
    print(label, "| changed:", out != text, "| findings:", findings)
Path("f.yaml").write_text("items:\n  - name: a\n    _target_: collections.Counter\n    size: 2\n")
print(convert_file("f.yaml", dry_run=True))
```
**Actual output:**
```
item, _target_ not first | changed: False | findings: []
multi-line flow         | changed: False | findings: []
quoted key with colon   | changed: False | findings: []
Result(path=PosixPath('f.yaml'), written=False, findings=[], activations_checked=0)
```
**Expected:** either converted or a `Finding(path, line, text, reason)` per site — AGENTS: "(1) what the grammar cannot convert is REPORTED as a `Finding(path, line, text, reason)` and left in place — never guessed at"; `docs/plain-format.md`: "what the grammar cannot convert is REPORTED, never guessed". A list of markers whose `_target_` is not the first key is an ordinary hand-written plain file.
**Known?** NEW

```python
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
cases = {"dw.yaml": "a: [1, 2]\na.0: 9\n", "dp.yaml": "a: ${X}\na.k: 9\n", "emb.yaml": "p: !class:collections.Counter\nx: pre-${ref:p}\n",
         "clone.yaml": "x: ${clone:p}\n", "imp.yaml": "x: 1\nimport: 42\n", "inc.yaml": "x: 1\ninclude: {path: b.yaml}\n", "qs.yaml": "m: '!class:Foo'\n"}
for name, text in cases.items():
    Path(name).write_text(text)
    try:
        load(name)
    except Exception as e:
        msg = str(e).replace(str(Path.cwd()), "<cwd>")
        print(f"{name:10} names file: {name in msg!s:5} line: {':2:' in msg or ':1:' in msg!s:5} | {msg.splitlines()[0][:80]}")
```
**Actual output:**
```
dw.yaml    names file: False line: False | the dotted key 'a.0' steps into a LIST at 'a' — a dotted write cannot index a li
dp.yaml    names file: False line: False | the dotted key 'a.k' writes through 'a', which holds the unresolved placeholder 
emb.yaml   names file: False line: False | ${ref:p} cannot be embedded in a string — a reference must be the whole value (g
clone.yaml names file: False line: False | ${clone:...} was removed (2026-08-15) — it had no users, and independence has on
imp.yaml   names file: False line: False | import: takes a module name or a list of names, got 42
inc.yaml   names file: True  line: False | include: takes a path or a list of paths, got {'path': 'b.yaml'} (in <cwd>/inc.y
qs.yaml    names file: False line: False | '!class:Foo' is a marker written as a quoted STRING, which confluid does not par
```
**Expected:** at minimum the file name (in hand as `base_path` at `loader.py:1254-1261`) on every message; `import:`/`include:` could carry the key's line from `map_constructor` — AGENTS: "EVERY error raised while processing a document NAMES ITS `file:line:col` … A config error without a location is not a usable error". The `qs.yaml` row is ruled and is not counted here; its "tracked in `TASKS.md`" claim is stale.
**Known?** NEW (the ruled quoted-string site excluded; record 21 defers the dotted-write locations "when mapping keys ever carry them" — the FILE name needs no key positions)
## PA30 · S4 — FIXED 2026-08-22 (docs/interpolation.md states the CD5 rule) — `docs/interpolation.md` says re-declaring a node with `!class:` in an overlay "is a replacement, and the overlay's marker wins outright"; since CD5 the code TUNES a same-target re-declaration
**Where:** `docs/interpolation.md:132-134` — "Re-declaring the node instead — writing `!class:` again in the overlay — is a replacement, and the overlay's marker wins outright." vs `confluid/merger.py:102-115` (the CD5 same-target tune arm: `tuned.kwargs = deep_merge(existing.kwargs, value.kwargs)`).
**Repro:**
```python
# PA30 — docs: re-declaring a node with `!class:` in an overlay "is a replacement"; the code TUNES it
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class Counter:
    def __init__(self, red=0, blue=0): self.red, self.blue = red, blue
    def __repr__(self): return f"Counter(red={self.red}, blue={self.blue})"
Path("base.yaml").write_text("model: !class:Counter\n  red: 1\n")
print(load("include: base.yaml\nmodel: !class:Counter\n  blue: 3\n"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
{'model': Counter(red=1, blue=3)}
```
**Expected:** per the docs `Counter(red=0, blue=3)`; per AGENTS (CD5, "same target + same marker kind → kwargs merge") `Counter(red=1, blue=3)`. One of the two must change — a reader following the docs to RESET a node's kwargs (drop the base's `red`) has no working spelling and no warning.
**Known?** NEW (docs drift after the 2026-08-20 CD5 change)
## PA31 · S4 — A FALSY document root (`0`, `false`, `[]`) loads as `{}`; `1` and `[1]` pass through
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:902` — `data = yaml.load(f, Loader=ConfluidLoader) or {}` and `:1260` — `data = yaml.load(str(data), Loader=ConfluidLoader) or {}`: the `or {}` meant for an EMPTY stream (`None`) also swallows every falsy root.
**Repro:**
```python
# PA31 — a FALSY document root (`0`, `false`, `[]`) loads as `{}`
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
for text in ("0", "false", "[]", "1", "[1]"):
    print(f"{text!r:8} ->", repr(load(text)))
Path("empty_list.yaml").write_text("[]\n")
print("file []  ->", repr(load("empty_list.yaml")))
```
**Actual output:**
```
'0'      -> {}
'false'  -> {}
'[]'     -> {}
'1'      -> 1
'[1]'    -> [1]
file []  -> {}
```
**Expected:** `0`, `False`, `[]` — record 20: "`load` accepts a path, YAML text or already-parsed data of any shape … a list root builds"; `tests/test_load_stages.py::test_a_scalar_root_passes_through_at_every_stage` pins pass-through for a (truthy) scalar. An empty list root becoming a dict is a type change the caller cannot see.
**Known?** NEW

---

### Suspected, not reproduced — PA

- **`_dotted_out_positioned` stamp leaking from a `load(doc)` copy into the caller's marker** — NOT reproduced: `document_copy`'s shallow `copy()` shares the attribute, but `expand_dotted_mapping` re-reads it through `getattr(…) or {}`, so an empty stamp dict is replaced, not mutated (`PA/work/p03_merger.py` G: caller `{}` before and after).
- **Cross-talk between concurrent `load(…, return_paths=True)` calls** — NOT reproduced (8 threads × 50 loads of two include trees, every result correct; the accumulator is a `ContextVar`).
- **`scopes._DECLARATION_LOCS` is a module global shared across threads** (`scopes.py:187`, "single-threaded within one load") — not probed; `scopes.py` is outside this area.
- **A top-level `scopes:` key is silently stripped** (`scopes: [train, val]` → gone at `until="document"`) — reproduced, but `docs/scopes.md` documents the key as "reserved for documenting a config's available scopes"; not counted (a footgun, not a contract breach).
- **`${a.m}` naming a MARKER resolves to the marker object** — reproduced; listed as "not counted" in BUGS-2026-08-19's suspected section, left there.
- **`!class:Bóx` (non-ASCII tag target) → raw `ScannerError`; a `---` multi-document file → raw `ComposerError`** — PyYAML's own grammar errors, which the prior report ruled PyYAML's to raise (CD16 renders them at the CLI); not counted.
- **Top-level int keys of a scope-block body are `str()`-ed** (`tbl: !scope:v2 {1: DJI}` → `{'1': 'DJI'}` at the parent, both spellings) — reproduced; the wrapper key vanishes so the idiom has no sensible use; not counted.
- **`${X:~}` → `None`, `${X:off}` → `False`** — the default rides `parse_value`'s YAML-1.1 coercion; same family as BUGS-2026-08-13 P14, not refiled.
- **`${.a}` resolves (leading dot tolerated by `_PATH_TOKEN_RE`) while `${a.}` stays literal** — harmless asymmetry, not counted.
- **`convert_file` on a file the loader REFUSES (`_partial_: 'true'`) raises `ConfigurationError` out of the equivalence gate instead of returning a `Finding`** — the source is invalid, so raising is defensible; not counted.
- **`.env` value `DATA_ROOT=$HOME/data` is not expanded** — python-dotenv expands `${HOME}` only; not confluid's.

---

# S· scopes & references — scopes.py / resolver.py / fluid.py (pass 4, `!ref:` / `${ref:}`)
## SR1 · S3 — A scoped `include:` that declares the activated dimension makes EVERY activation of that dimension a `ScopeError` — the declared-value check re-runs on each settle pass against a document whose blocks have changed

**Where:** `confluid/scopes.py:176` — `_check_active_values_are_declared(config, active)` runs inside `resolve_scopes`, which `confluid/loader.py:1041-1042` calls once PER settle pass (`for _ in range(_MAX_SETTLE_PASSES): data = resolve_scopes(data, active)`). Pass 1 sees only the entry file's blocks (a value declared inside the not-yet-opened include is "undeclared"); pass 2 sees only the blocks the include just spliced (the entry file's blocks were consumed in pass 1), so a value declared in the entry file is now "undeclared".

**Repro:**
```python
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
# (a) value declared ONLY in the entry file, include declares another value of the same dimension
Path("sub.yaml").write_text("other: !scope:fw=y\n  v: 1\nplain: 7\n")
Path("main.yaml").write_text("a: !scope:fw=x\n  include: sub.yaml\n")
try:
    print(load("main.yaml", scopes=["fw=x"], until="document"))
except Exception as e:
    print(type(e).__name__ + ":", e)
# (b) the two-dimension shape — a framework overlay declaring its own model variants
Path("torch.yaml").write_text("big: !scope:model=big\n  width: 64\nsmall: !scope:model=small\n  width: 8\n")
Path("main2.yaml").write_text("tiny: !scope:model=tiny\n  width: 2\ntorch: !scope:fw=torch\n  include: torch.yaml\n")
for sc in (["fw=torch", "model=big"], ["fw=torch", "model=tiny"]):
    try: print(sc, "->", load("main2.yaml", scopes=sc, until="document"))
    except Exception as e: print(sc, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:200])
```
**Actual output:**
```
ScopeError: No scope block matches fw='x'. This document declares fw with: y (declared at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/scratchpad/hunt/SR/work/final/SR1/sub.yaml:1:8). Either use one of those values, or add a `_scope_: {fw: x}` block.
['fw=torch', 'model=big'] -> ScopeError No scope block matches model='big'. This document declares model with: tiny (declared at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/scratchpad/hunt/SR/work/final/S
['fw=torch', 'model=tiny'] -> ScopeError No scope block matches model='tiny'. This document declares model with: big, small (declared at /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/scratchpad/hunt/SR/work/
```
(In (a) the activation `fw=x` DID select a block — the one that spliced `sub.yaml` — and is then refused by the second pass, which only sees `sub.yaml`'s `fw=y`. In (b) neither `model=big` nor `model=tiny` is loadable: `big` is refused in pass 1, `tiny` in pass 2.)
**Expected:** `{'v': 1, 'plain': 7}` for (a); `{'width': 64}` / `{'width': 2}` for (b) — `docs/scopes.md`: "A keyed activation must name a value the document actually carries"; `docs/interpolation.md` → Conditional includes: "The alternation repeats, so an included file may itself contain a scope block carrying a further include." Both values ARE carried by the composed document; the check must see the whole set of blocks once, not a different subset per pass.
**Known?** NEW

---
**Same root as:** PA7 (the declared-value check re-running per settle pass) — folded into this entry.

## SR2 · S2 — `default_scopes:` / `scope_aliases:` declared in a file a SCOPE BLOCK splices are silently stripped without being read (a plain `include:` of the same file honours them)

**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:1289-1293` reads `scope_aliases` / `default_scopes` ONCE, before `_settle_scopes_and_includes`; a scoped include lands its top-level keys in pass 2, where `confluid/scopes.py:178-179` (`resolved = {k: v for k, v in resolved.items() if k not in METADATA_KEYS}`) strips them on the next `resolve_scopes` without anything reading them.

**Repro:**
```python
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load
Path("defaults.yaml").write_text("default_scopes: [model=big]\nbig: !scope:model=big\n  width: 64\nsmall: !scope:model=small\n  width: 8\n")
Path("main2.yaml").write_text("torch: !scope:fw=torch\n  include: defaults.yaml\n")
Path("main3.yaml").write_text("include: defaults.yaml\n")
print("plain include   :", load("main3.yaml", until="document"))
print("scoped include  :", load("main2.yaml", scopes=["fw=torch"], until="document"))
Path("al.yaml").write_text("scope_aliases: {ci: [quick]}\nq: !scope:quick\n  q: 1\n")
Path("main_al.yaml").write_text("t: !scope:fw=x\n  include: al.yaml\n")
Path("main_al2.yaml").write_text("include: al.yaml\n")
print("alias via plain include :", load("main_al2.yaml", scopes=["ci"], until="document"))
print("alias via scoped include:", load("main_al.yaml", scopes=["fw=x", "ci"], until="document"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
plain include   : {'width': 64}
scoped include  : {}
alias via plain include : {'q': 1}
alias via scoped include: {}
```
**Expected:** `{'width': 64}` and `{'q': 1}` through the scoped include too — `docs/interpolation.md`: "An `include:` behaves as if the included document were pasted into your file at that line. That is the whole rule." and "A scope block splices by the same rule." The defaulted variant is neither selected nor refused; the run proceeds on nothing.
**Known?** NEW (the 2026-08-19 "Suspected" list notes metadata written INSIDE a block; this is metadata at the TOP LEVEL of an included file, which the plain include honours.)

---
## SR3 · S2 — Kwargs on a reference are silently DROPPED when the referent lives in an enclosing mapping other than the immediate parent or the root (pass 7 resolves the reference; the fold never finds it)

**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:485-489` — `fold_reference_kwargs.referent_of` probes `for ctx in (local, document)` (the immediate enclosing mapping and the root) and returns `None` otherwise, so the kwargs stay on the `Reference`; `confluid/engine.py:582-584` then resolves the same reference from the enclosing VIEW (which carries every ancestor's keys), returns the settled referent and never reads `ref.kwargs`.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, v=1, k=0, use=None): self.v, self.k, self.use = v, k, use
doc = "outer:\n  proto: !class:S\n    v: 1\n  inner:\n    use: !ref:proto\n      k: 9\n"
print("settled:", load(doc, until="settled"))
o = load(doc)
print("objects: proto.k =", o["outer"]["proto"].k, "| use.k =", o["outer"]["inner"]["use"].k, "| same:", o["outer"]["inner"]["use"] is o["outer"]["proto"])
doc2 = "proto: !class:S\n  v: 1\ninner:\n  use: !ref:proto\n    k: 9\n"          # control: referent at the ROOT
o2 = load(doc2)
print("control: proto.k =", o2["proto"].k, "| use.k =", o2["inner"]["use"].k)
o = load("outer:\n  proto: !class:S\n    v: 1\n  m: !class:S\n    use: !ref:proto\n      k: 9\n")   # same, inside a marker's kwargs
print("marker-kwargs variant: proto.k =", o["outer"]["proto"].k, "| m.use is proto:", o["outer"]["m"].use is o["outer"]["proto"])
```
**Actual output:**
```
settled: {'outer': {'proto': Target(S, {'v': 1}), 'inner': {'use': Target(S, {'v': 1})}}}
objects: proto.k = 0 | use.k = 0 | same: True
control: proto.k = 9 | use.k = 9
marker-kwargs variant: proto.k = 0 | m.use is proto: True
```
**Expected:** `proto.k = 9` in every row — `AGENTS.md` → "kwargs on a reference TUNE the shared referent, folded BEFORE pass 7 … Resolution mirrors `_settle_reference`"; `_settle_reference` is "nearest enclosing scope first … then the root" and it DOES find `proto` two levels up, so the two resolutions do not mirror each other. No warning, empty report: the author's `k: 9` vanishes.
**Known?** NEW

---
## SR4 · S2 — Kwargs on a DOTTED or INDEXED reference are silently dropped, in all three spellings

**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:486` — `if target in ctx and ctx[target] is not node:` — the fold's referent lookup is an EXACT-key probe, so `grp.proto` / `items[0]` never resolve and the kwargs stay on the `Reference`; `confluid/engine.py:591-598` then walks the path structurally and inlines the settled referent without its kwargs.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, v=1, k=0): self.v, self.k = v, k
for label, doc in [
    ("dotted ref + body      ", "grp:\n  proto: !class:S\n    v: 1\nuse: !ref:grp.proto\n  k: 9\n"),
    ("dotted _ref_ + kwargs  ", "grp:\n  proto: {_target_: S, v: 1}\nuse: {_ref_: grp.proto, k: 9}\n"),
    ("dotted ${ref} + use.k  ", "grp:\n  proto: !class:S\n    v: 1\nuse: ${ref:grp.proto}\nuse.k: 9\n"),
    ("list-index ref + body  ", "items:\n  - !class:S\n    v: 1\nuse: !ref:items[0]\n  k: 9\n"),
    ("control exact ref+body ", "proto: !class:S\n  v: 1\nuse: !ref:proto\n  k: 9\n"),
]:
    try:
        o = load(doc)
        print(label, "-> use.k =", o["use"].k, "| settled:", load(doc, until="settled")["use"])
    except Exception as e:
        print(label, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:160])
```
**Actual output:**
```
dotted ref + body       -> use.k = 0 | settled: Target(S, {'v': 1})
dotted _ref_ + kwargs   -> use.k = 0 | settled: Target(S, {'v': 1})
dotted ${ref} + use.k   -> use.k = 0 | settled: Target(S, {'v': 1})
list-index ref + body   -> use.k = 0 | settled: Target(S, {'v': 1})
control exact ref+body  -> use.k = 9 | settled: Target(S, {'v': 1, 'k': 9})
```
**Expected:** `use.k = 9` for every row — `docs/plain-format.md`: "the mapping form when you want to tune the shared object from the reference: `{_ref_: proto, k: 5}` folds `k: 5` into `proto`'s own kwargs (every alias sees it)"; `docs/targets.md`: a dotted `!ref:` "walks structure only — dict keys and list indices (`!ref:cfg.lr`, `!ref:packs[1].name`)". Nothing says the tune is exact-key only; the kwargs are discarded without a word (and `hydraide emit` emits the referent without them, so `check` cannot catch it either).
**Known?** NEW (PA10/BC8/SR9 fixed the exact-key spellings only)

---
**Same root as:** PA6 (`fold_reference_kwargs`' exact-key referent probe) — folded into this entry.

## SR5 · S3 — A dotted reference is refused as an "attribute reference" when its first key exists at the ROOT but the structural walk only succeeds in the ENCLOSING scope (nearest-scope rule inverted for plain values)

**Where:** `confluid/engine.py:590` — `refuse_attribute_reference(target, root if _first_key(target) in root else scope, _at_yaml_loc(ref))` probes the ROOT whenever the first key exists there, although `:591-592` (`for ctx in (scope, root): found = Resolver(context=ctx)._lookup_path_found(target, ctx)`) would try the SCOPE first one line later.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, v=1): self.v = v
for label, doc, kw in [
    ("shadowed cfg, scope has b ", "cfg: {a: 1}\ngrp:\n  cfg: {b: 2}\n  use: !ref:cfg.b\n", {}),
    ("shadowed cfg, settled     ", "cfg: {a: 1}\ngrp:\n  cfg: {b: 2}\n  use: !ref:cfg.b\n", {"until": "settled"}),
    ("control: root only        ", "grp:\n  cfg: {b: 2}\n  use: !ref:cfg.b\n", {}),
    ("control: exact key shadow ", "cfg: {a: 1}\ngrp:\n  cfg: {b: 2}\n  use: !ref:cfg\n", {}),
]:
    try: print(label, "->", load(doc, **kw))
    except Exception as e: print(label, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:200])
o = load("cfg: {a: 1}\ngrp:\n  cfg:\n    b: !class:S\n  use: !ref:cfg.b\n")   # con: a MARKER at the path — pass 6 inlines it, so pass 7 never sees the reference
print("con (marker at scope path): use is grp.cfg.b:", o["grp"]["use"] is o["grp"]["cfg"]["b"])
```
**Actual output:**
```
shadowed cfg, scope has b  -> ConfigurationError !ref:cfg.b at <unicode string>:4:8 reads the ATTRIBUTE `b` of the object built at `cfg` — attribute references were removed (2026-08, architecture record 19). Read the attribute on the consumer side: 
shadowed cfg, settled      -> ConfigurationError !ref:cfg.b at <unicode string>:4:8 reads the ATTRIBUTE `b` of the object built at `cfg` — attribute references were removed (2026-08, architecture record 19). Read the attribute on the consumer side: 
control: root only         -> {'grp': {'cfg': {'b': 2}, 'use': 2}}
control: exact key shadow  -> {'cfg': {'a': 1}, 'grp': {'cfg': {'b': 2}, 'use': {'b': 2}}}
con (marker at scope path): use is grp.cfg.b: True
```
**Expected:** `use: 2` — `AGENTS.md` → "a reference is settled ONCE, in pass 7": "Nearest enclosing scope first (an included fragment's internal `!ref:` finds the fragment's key), then the root". The exact-key spelling (`!ref:cfg`) honours the rule; the dotted spelling of the same lookup is refused with a message about an object that does not exist (`cfg` is a plain dict on both levels). The shape is exactly an included fragment with its own `cfg:` section next to a root `cfg:`.
**Known?** NEW

---
## SR6 · S2 — A document whose ROOT is a marker or a list is never scope-resolved when no `scopes=` is passed: the `ScopeBlock` leaks as a constructor kwarg / list item, `!notscope:` never splices, and `hydraide` emits `_target_: ScopeBlock`

**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:1289-1297` — `if isinstance(data, dict): … _settle_scopes_and_includes(…) elif scopes: …` — a non-dict root runs pass 4 ONLY when the caller passes a non-empty `scopes` list.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, configurable
from confluid.hydraide import emit
@configurable
class X:
    def __init__(self, v=0, blk=None): self.v, self.blk = v, blk
for label, doc, kw in [
    ("root marker, notscope, no scopes ", "!class:X\n  blk: !notscope:debug\n    v: 5\n", {}),
    ("root marker, notscope, scopes=[] ", "!class:X\n  blk: !notscope:debug\n    v: 5\n", {"scopes": []}),
    ("root marker, notscope, scopes=[q]", "!class:X\n  blk: !notscope:debug\n    v: 5\n", {"scopes": ["q"]}),
    ("root marker, scope, no scopes    ", "!class:X\n  blk: !scope:debug\n    v: 5\n", {}),
    ("root list, notscope, no scopes   ", "- a\n- !notscope:debug\n  - b\n- c\n", {"until": "document"}),
    ("root list, notscope, scopes=[q]  ", "- a\n- !notscope:debug\n  - b\n- c\n", {"until": "document", "scopes": ["q"]}),
    ("dict control, notscope, no scopes", "m: !class:X\n  blk: !notscope:debug\n    v: 5\n", {}),
]:
    r = load(doc, **kw)
    print(label, "->", "X(v=%r, blk=%r)" % (r.v, r.blk) if isinstance(r, X) else r)
for doc in ("!class:X\n  blk: !notscope:debug\n    v: 5\n", "- a\n- !notscope:debug\n  - b\n"):
    print(repr(doc), "-> emit:", repr(emit(doc)))
```
**Actual output:**
```
root marker, notscope, no scopes  -> X(v=0, blk=_notscope_: {debug} {'v': 5})
root marker, notscope, scopes=[]  -> X(v=0, blk=_notscope_: {debug} {'v': 5})
root marker, notscope, scopes=[q] -> X(v=5, blk=None)
root marker, scope, no scopes     -> X(v=0, blk=_scope_: {debug} {'v': 5})
root list, notscope, no scopes    -> ['a', _notscope_: {debug} ['b'], 'c']
root list, notscope, scopes=[q]   -> ['a', 'b', 'c']
dict control, notscope, no scopes -> {'m': <__main__.X object at 0x10af4f800>}
'!class:X\n  blk: !notscope:debug\n    v: 5\n' -> emit: '_target_: X\nblk:\n  _target_: ScopeBlock\n  dims:\n    debug: null\n  negate: true\n  contents:\n    v: 5\n'
'- a\n- !notscope:debug\n  - b\n' -> emit: '- a\n- _target_: ScopeBlock\n  dims:\n    debug: null\n  negate: true\n  contents:\n  - b\n'
```
**Expected:** `X(v=5, blk=None)` and `['a', 'b', 'c']` whatever `scopes=` says (an unrelated activation `["q"]` must not be what makes the document correct) — `docs/scopes.md`: "Where a scope block may live: Anywhere a mapping key does — the document root, a nested dict, a list item, and inside a marker's own block" and "`_notscope_` is also active when the user passes no `--KEY ...` at all (the unset ⇒ active convention)". The emitted artefact (`_target_: ScopeBlock`) is not reloadable.
**Known?** overlaps P3 (BUGS-2026-08-13, OPEN — the root-`ScopeBlock` shape of the same `elif scopes:` branch). This entry is the root-MARKER / root-LIST shape, which P3 does not cover.

## SR7 · S4 — FIXED 2026-08-22 (`tests/test_scopes.py`) — A dimension declared by BOTH a boolean block and keyed blocks refuses the bare activation as "selects nothing", although the boolean block would fire

**Where:** `confluid/scopes.py:295-308` — `if key in negated or not positive.get(key): continue` then `if value is None: raise ScopeError("… a bare activation selects nothing …")`; the boolean block (`dims == {debug: None}`) is skipped by `_walk_dimensions` (`:208-209`), so the check cannot see that it exists, while `_is_active` (`:329-330`, `if value is None: return key in active`) would fire it.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, discover_dimension_values
doc = "b: !scope:debug\n  log: DEBUG\nk: !scope:debug=verbose\n  log: TRACE\n"
print("discover:", discover_dimension_values(load(doc, until="raw")))
for sc in (["debug"], ["debug=verbose"], []):
    try: print(sc, "->", load(doc, scopes=sc, until="document"))
    except Exception as e: print(sc, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:200])
```
**Actual output:**
```
discover: {'debug': {'verbose'}}
['debug'] -> ScopeError debug is a KEYED dimension — a bare activation selects nothing. This document declares debug with: verbose (declared at <unicode string>:3:4); write debug=<value>.
['debug=verbose'] -> {'log': 'TRACE'}
[] -> {}
```
**Expected:** `['debug'] -> {'log': 'DEBUG'}` — `docs/scopes.md`: "Boolean — flips on with `--scope debug`"; the refusal's own justification ("a bare name can select nothing") is false for this document. (The SR13 fix is correct for a keyed-only dimension; this is the mixed case it did not consider.)
**Known?** NEW

## SR9 · S2 — A TAG-spelled scope block whose body carries a reserved key directly (`_target_:` / `_ref_:` / `_partial_:`) splices the reserved key into the parent as literal DATA; the reserved-key spelling of the same node is refused

**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:722` — `contents = _str_keyed_mapping(loader, node)` in `_build_scope`; `_refuse_reserved_keys_in_tag_body` is deliberately not called for scope tags (`:639-641`: "a scope block's body keys are ordinary config keys and may legitimately splice a `_target_:` mapping" — true for a NESTED mapping, which `map_constructor` converts, false for a key at the body's own top level, which nothing converts).

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, v=1): self.v = v
for label, doc in [
    ("tag body _target_       ", "model: !scope:x\n  _target_: S\n  v: 2\n"),
    ("reserved body _target_  ", "model:\n  _scope_: {x: }\n  _target_: S\n  v: 2\n"),
    ("tag body _ref_          ", "proto: !class:S\nuse: !scope:x\n  _ref_: proto\n"),
    ("tag body _partial_      ", "use: !scope:x\n  _partial_: true\n  v: 2\n"),
    ("tag notscope _target_   ", "model: !notscope:x\n  _target_: S\n  v: 2\n"),
]:
    for sc in (["x"], []):
        try: print(label, sc, "->", load(doc, scopes=sc, until="document"))
        except Exception as e: print(label, sc, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:120])
```
**Actual output:**
```
tag body _target_        ['x'] -> {'_target_': 'S', 'v': 2}
tag body _target_        [] -> {}
reserved body _target_   ['x'] -> ConfigurationError Conflicting reserved keys in one mapping: _scope_, _target_ (at <unicode string>:2:3)
reserved body _target_   [] -> ConfigurationError Conflicting reserved keys in one mapping: _scope_, _target_ (at <unicode string>:2:3)
tag body _ref_           ['x'] -> {'proto': Target(S, {}), '_ref_': 'proto'}
tag body _ref_           [] -> {'proto': Target(S, {})}
tag body _partial_       ['x'] -> {'_partial_': True, 'v': 2}
tag body _partial_       [] -> {}
tag notscope _target_    ['x'] -> {}
tag notscope _target_    [] -> {'_target_': 'S', 'v': 2}
```
**Expected:** the same located `ConfigurationError` the reserved-key spelling raises — `AGENTS.md` → "Two spellings, ONE intermediate representation": "Both MUST produce the SAME Fluid markers. A behaviour reachable from only one spelling is a BUG in that spelling" and "A malformed marker raises a located `ConfigurationError` at load. Never degrade one silently". A literal `_target_` key at the document's top level is exactly the degradation the format exists to end (P16's mechanism, one spelling over).
**Known?** NEW

## SR10 · S2 — A dotted reference THROUGH a section's own internal reference resolves that inner reference in the REFERRER's scope: the wrong object when the root shadows the name, a located crash when it does not

**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:566-568` — `res = self._resolve_ref(value.target, local_context, exclude=value)` finds the inner `Reference` object at `sub.use`, then `return self.resolve(res, local_context)` re-resolves it against the OUTER reference's `local_context` (the root) instead of the scope the inner reference was written in.

**Repro:**
```python
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import load, configurable
@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, v=1): self.v = v
Path("frag.yaml").write_text("proto: !class:S\n  v: 2\nuse: !ref:proto\n")
Path("main.yaml").write_text("proto: !class:S\n  v: 1\nsub:\n  include: frag.yaml\nmain_use: !ref:sub.use\n")
o = load("main.yaml")
print("sub.use is sub.proto:", o["sub"]["use"] is o["sub"]["proto"], "(v=%d)" % o["sub"]["use"].v)
print("main_use.v =", o["main_use"].v, "| main_use is sub.proto:", o["main_use"] is o["sub"]["proto"], "| main_use is ROOT proto:", o["main_use"] is o["proto"])
doc = "proto: !class:S\n  v: 1\nsub:\n  proto: !class:S\n    v: 2\n  use: !ref:proto\nmain_use: !ref:sub.use\n"   # same thing in ONE file
o = load(doc)
print("one file: sub.use.v =", o["sub"]["use"].v, "| main_use.v =", o["main_use"].v, "| main_use is sub.proto:", o["main_use"] is o["sub"]["proto"])
print("document stage:", load(doc, until="document"))
doc2 = "sub:\n  proto: !class:S\n    v: 2\n  use: !ref:proto\nmain_use: !ref:sub.use\n"   # control: no root proto at all
o = load(doc2)
```
**Actual output:**
```
sub.use is sub.proto: True (v=2)
main_use.v = 1 | main_use is sub.proto: False | main_use is ROOT proto: True
one file: sub.use.v = 2 | main_use.v = 1 | main_use is sub.proto: False
document stage: {'proto': Target(S, {'v': 1}), 'sub': {'proto': Target(S, {'v': 2}), 'use': Target(S, {'v': 2})}, 'main_use': Target(S, {'v': 1})}
Traceback (most recent call last):
  File "/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/scratchpad/hunt/SR/work/final/SR10/probe.py", line 16, in <module>
    o = load(doc2)
        ^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/Users/gert/taidal/confluid/confluid/loader.py", line 1202, in load
    return _load(data, until=until, context=context, scopes=scopes, solidify=solidify)
           ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/Users/gert/taidal/confluid/confluid/loader.py", line 1329, in _load
    return materialize(data, context=ctx, solidify=solidify)
           ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/Users/gert/taidal/confluid/confluid/engine.py", line 190, in materialize
    result = _flow_recursive(data, parent_context=context)
             ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/Users/gert/taidal/confluid/confluid/engine.py", line 391, in _flow_recursive
    return {k: _flow_recursive(v, parent_context=local_ctx, slot_key=k) for k, v in data.items()}
               ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/Users/gert/taidal/confluid/confluid/engine.py", line 551, in _flow_recursive
    return _settle_reference(data, parent_context)
           ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/Users/gert/taidal/confluid/confluid/engine.py", line 602, in _settle_reference
    raise ReferenceResolutionError(
confluid.exceptions.ReferenceResolutionError: Cannot resolve !ref:proto at <unicode string>:4:8: no key in scope, no structural path and no importable name matches it.
```
(`sub.use` itself resolves correctly to `sub.proto` (v=2); `main_use: !ref:sub.use` — the SAME node, read through a dotted path — is the root `proto` (v=1). Without a root `proto` the load crashes on a document every key of which is resolvable.)
**Expected:** `main_use is sub.proto` (v=2) in both files, and `doc2` loads — `AGENTS.md`: "Nearest enclosing scope first (an included fragment's internal `!ref:` finds the fragment's key), then the root"; `docs/targets.md`: "a marker reached directly or through any number of `!ref:` resolves to one live instance". The fragment's reference must mean the same thing whoever reads it.
**Known?** NEW

## SR11 · S3 — An EMPTY `!ref:` path resolves to the WHOLE DOCUMENT in pass 6 and dies with a raw `RecursionError` in pass 7; `${ref:}` stays a literal string; only `_ref_: ''` is refused — three spellings, three behaviours

**Where:** `confluid/resolver.py:187-208` — `_parse_path_segments("")` returns `[]` (the `while pos < n` loop never runs), and `:240-285` `_walk_path_segments([], context, …)` returns `context` itself, so `_lookup_path("")` answers the root mapping; `confluid/engine.py:592-598` then `_flow_recursive`s that mapping, which contains the reference, forever. `${ref:}` never matches `_INTERP_RE` (`:55`, the default group needs one character) so `_marker_resolver`'s "needs a target path" refusal at `:835-836` is unreachable from that spelling.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load, configurable
from confluid.resolver import Resolver, _parse_path_segments, _walk_path_segments
@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, v=1): self.v = v
print("segments('') =", _parse_path_segments(""), "| walk([], {'a': 1}) =", _walk_path_segments([], {"a": 1}, lambda n, c: None))
print("_lookup_path('', {'a': 1}) =", Resolver()._lookup_path("", {"a": 1}))
for label, doc, kw in [
    ("root, document stage ", "a: 1\nuse: !ref:\n", {"until": "document"}),
    ("root, settled        ", "a: 1\nuse: !ref:\n", {"until": "settled"}),
    ("in marker kwargs     ", "m: !class:S\n  v: !ref:\n", {}),
    ("${ref:} placeholder  ", "use: ${ref:}\n", {"until": "settled"}),
    ("_ref_: ''            ", "use: {_ref_: ''}\n", {"until": "settled"}),
]:
    try: print(label, "->", load(doc, **kw))
    except RecursionError as e: print(label, "-> RecursionError", str(e)[:60])
    except Exception as e: print(label, "->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:120])
```
**Actual output:**
```
segments('') = [] | walk([], {'a': 1}) = {'a': 1}
_lookup_path('', {'a': 1}) = {'a': 1}
root, document stage  -> {'a': 1, 'use': Reference(, {})}
root, settled         -> RecursionError maximum recursion depth exceeded
in marker kwargs      -> RecursionError maximum recursion depth exceeded
${ref:} placeholder   -> {'use': '${ref:}'}
_ref_: ''             -> ConfigurationError _ref_ must be a non-empty string path (got '') (at <unicode string>:1:6)
```
**Expected:** the located `ConfigurationError` the `_ref_` spelling raises, from all three — `AGENTS.md`: "A malformed marker raises a located `ConfigurationError` at load. Never degrade one silently"; "Every error confluid raises MUST come from `confluid/exceptions.py` … never a bare builtin". `${ref:}` reaching a constructor as the literal string `'${ref:}'` is the silent half; the `RecursionError` is the untyped, unlocated half.
**Known?** NEW

## SR12 · S4 — Three scope errors that have a file or a YAML node in hand name none of it: the alias-cycle `ScopeError`, the call-form refusal raised from a `!scope:` TAG, and a `default_scopes` error that names a file that does not exist

**Where:** `confluid/scopes.py:468` — `raise ScopeError(f"Circular scope alias detected: …")` with no `where`, although `confluid/loader.py:1291` computes `where` two lines above the call and passes it only to `parse_default_scopes`; `confluid/loader.py:711` — `_parse_scope_suffix(tag_suffix)` raises `scopes.py:58`'s call-form refusal BEFORE `_build_scope` reaches `_node_where(node, loader)` (`:717`), so the node's `file:line:col` is lost; `confluid/loader.py:1254` — `base_path = Path.cwd() / "string.yaml"` for TEXT input, which `:1291` renders as the `where` prefix.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import load
cases = [
 ("alias cycle          ", "scope_aliases: {a: b, b: a}\nq: !scope:x\n  v: 1\n", ["a"]),
 ("call-form tag        ", "q: !scope:task(cls)\n  v: 1\n", []),
 ("bad default_scopes   ", "default_scopes: fw=a\nq: !scope:fw=a\n  v: 1\n", []),
]
for label, doc, sc in cases:
    try:
        load(doc, scopes=sc, until="settled")
    except Exception as e:
        msg = str(e).replace("\n", " ")
        print(label, "->", type(e).__name__, "| names <unicode string>:", "<unicode string>" in msg, "|", msg[:150])
```
**Actual output:**
```
alias cycle           -> ScopeError | names <unicode string>: False | Circular scope alias detected: a -> b -> a
call-form tag         -> ScopeError | names <unicode string>: False | scope activation 'task(cls)': the call form was removed — write 'task=cls'
bad default_scopes    -> ScopeError | names <unicode string>: False | /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/scratchpad/hunt/SR/work/final/SR12/string.yaml: `default_scopes` must 
```
(`…/p12/string.yaml` does not exist — the input was YAML text; the tag constructors' own diagnostics say `<unicode string>:line:col` for the same input.)
**Expected:** `… at <file>:1:4` for the tag (the node is in `_build_scope`'s hands), the file for the alias cycle (the same `where` `parse_default_scopes` gets), and `<unicode string>` / `<config>` rather than a phantom path — `AGENTS.md` → Typed exceptions: "EVERY error raised while processing a document NAMES ITS `file:line:col` … A helper that raises about a node MUST receive the NODE". The sibling `ScopeError`s (undeclared value, bare keyed, sequence body) were located by the SR14/SR15 sweep; these three were missed.
**Known?** NEW (SR15 located the undeclared-value and malformed-`default_scopes` messages; the phantom `string.yaml` is a side effect of that fix)
