## ENG-19 · S2 — `flow(Cls, solidify=False)` on a registry-configurable bare TYPE solidifies anyway (the marker spelling of the same call suppresses)
**Where:** `confluid/engine.py:1562` — `return materialize(marker, context=context)` — `_flow_bare_type` re-enters through `materialize`, whose fresh state sets `:185` `suppress_solidify=not solidify` with `solidify` defaulting to `True`, discarding the ambient suppression installed at `:658`.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, flow, Target
@configurable
class Model:
    def __init__(self): self.built = False
    def solidify(self): self.built = True
print("flow(Model, solidify=False).built         ->", flow(Model, solidify=False).built)
print("flow(Target(Model), solidify=False).built ->", flow(Target(Model), solidify=False).built)
```
**Actual output:**
```
flow(Model, solidify=False).built         -> True
flow(Target(Model), solidify=False).built -> False
```
**Expected:** `False` for both — `AGENTS.md` → Post-flow auto-solidification: "`flow(obj, solidify=False)` / `load(..., solidify=False)` suppress it for the whole subtree."
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-13 E9 (open, not marked FIXED — re-verified 2026-08-22, still reproduces)

---
## ENG-20 · S3 — A BARE key colliding with a `*args` name REFUSES the whole load when the class also takes `**kwargs` (the exemption is structural only for classes WITH an accept-list)
**Where:** `confluid/engine.py:1241` — `refuse_if_variadic_name(target, k, obj)` — reached for a bare key because a `**kwargs` class has no accept-list (`None` = accept everything), so the "accept-list drops the key before either call site" exemption never fires.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, load
@configurable(validate=False)
class Pipeline:
    def __init__(self, *stages, **options): self.options = options
@configurable(validate=False)
class Pipeline2:
    def __init__(self, *stages): pass
for cls in ("Pipeline", "Pipeline2"):
    try: load(f"node: {{_target_: {cls}}}\nstages: 3\n"); print(cls, "(bare stages: 3) -> loads")
    except Exception as e: print(cls, "(bare stages: 3) ->", type(e).__name__, str(e)[:95], "...")
```
**Actual output:**
```
Pipeline (bare stages: 3) -> ConfigurationError Pipeline cannot accept 'stages' at <unicode string>:1:7: it is a *args parameter, which can nev ...
Pipeline2 (bare stages: 3) -> loads
```
**Expected:** both load — `AGENTS.md` → Runtime injection has a POSITIONAL channel: "BARE keys are EXEMPT and must stay so. A bare key is an implicit `**.key` that cascades tree-wide and legitimately matches nothing, so a document whose top-level key collides with some class's variadic name must keep loading."
**Known?** overlaps BUGS-2026-08-13 E5 (open, not marked FIXED — re-verified 2026-08-22, still reproduces)

---

### Suspected, not reproduced — ENG

- `__init__ = None` class: `load("x: {_target_: Nulled}")` returns the `Target` marker itself as the "object" (`engine.py:772` `if params is None: return obj`) — reproduced, but the docstring at `:1039-1049` calls the silent degradation deliberate and notes a located error "is a behaviour change … deliberately not made"; listed for the ruling, not filed.
- The decorator-side capture stamp (`decorators.py`, `__confluid_kwargs__` written inside the wrapped `__init__`) raises the same `ValueError` as ENG-10 for a validating `__setattr__` under the default `capture=True` — measured (`Failed to construct Strict at <unicode string>:1:4: Strict has no field '__confluid_kwargs__'`), located, but it makes such a class unloadable; outside this area.
- `_broadcast_onto_instance`'s second sweep (`:1505`) also triggers a dynamic `__getattr__` (Mock / RPC proxy) — same line as ENG-9, not separately run.
- `configure()` of a ctor-default `PartialClass` (ENG-1's class shape) tunes the SHARED class-level default marker in place (`kwargs.update`, identity kept), so every other host's default changes — reasoned from the configurator's in-place tune rule; configurator area, not run.
- A class-ATTRIBUTE marker default (`class Car: engine = Target(Engine)`) is never built or broadcast into (sweep 1 walks `__dict__`, sweep 2 walks ctor params) — odd spelling, not run.
- `_construct`'s rebuild `raise type(exc)(msg)` loses the located message for an exception class whose `__init__` ignores its argument (`def __init__(self, *a): pass`) — contrived, not run.
- `flow(Reference("opt"))` where the referent is a `_partial_` marker BUILDS it with no runtime args (rule 2: an explicit `flow()` builds) — by design, noted only.
- `eager=True` staleness warning fires on EVERY `configure()` call that sets a ctor param (measured twice for two calls) — no doc says "once"; configurator area, not filed.
- `materialize()` re-enters from `_flow_bare_type` inside a constructor during an outer pass: the nested `clear_pass_caches()` and fresh memos were measured harmless for the outer pass's identity sharing (a nested `load()` inside a constructor kept `a is b` for an anchored marker), so the X4 family is not re-opened.
- `flow(marker)` of a settled marker OUTSIDE any context builds shared `!ref:` children once per flow (no memo) — documented in `docs/concurrency.md` (`active_context` "installs fresh instance-sharing memos"); not filed.

---

# C· configure / dump / report / hydraide / CLI
## CD1 · S1 — A bare key written BEFORE a named overlay beats it under `configure()`; `load()` of the same document gives the overlay
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:143` — `document[k] = deep_merge({k: document[k]}, {k: v})[k]  # P1: an overlay mapping TUNES a marker` and `:148` — `merged = {**document, **rest}`. The overlay's keys are folded into the object's marker, which sits at the FRONT of the merged document (the objects come first), so every bare key in `rest` is positioned after it — whatever order the user wrote. The report then writes a second `applied` record for the same key on the same object (`:144` `_record_named_overlay`), and `explain` shows the overlay's value as `own`.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure, load

@configurable
class Trainer:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.001):
        self.lr = lr

cases = [
    ("bare first, named block later", "lr: 2.0\ntrainer: {lr: 1.0}\n",               "lr: 2.0\ntrainer: {_target_: Trainer, lr: 1.0}\n"),
    ("bare first, dotted later",      "lr: 2.0\ntrainer.lr: 1.0\n",                  "trainer: {_target_: Trainer}\nlr: 2.0\ntrainer.lr: 1.0\n"),
    ("bare first, marker later",      "lr: 2.0\ntrainer: !class:Trainer {lr: 1.0}\n", "lr: 2.0\ntrainer: !class:Trainer {lr: 1.0}\n"),
    ("named block first, bare later", "trainer: {lr: 1.0}\nlr: 2.0\n",               "trainer: {_target_: Trainer, lr: 1.0}\nlr: 2.0\n"),
]
for label, cfg, load_twin in cases:
    t = Trainer()
    report = configure(trainer=t, config=cfg)
    print(f"{label:32} configure -> {t.lr}   load -> {load(load_twin)['trainer'].lr}")
t = Trainer()
report = configure(trainer=t, config="lr: 2.0\ntrainer: {lr: 1.0}\n")
print("applied:", [(a.key, a.target, a.origin) for a in report.applied])
print(report.explain("lr"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
bare first, named block later    configure -> 2.0   load -> 1.0
bare first, dotted later         configure -> 2.0   load -> 1.0
bare first, marker later         configure -> 2.0   load -> 1.0
named block first, bare later    configure -> 2.0   load -> 2.0
applied: [('lr', "Trainer 'trainer'", 'addressed'), ('lr', 'Trainer', 'bare')]
lr on Trainer 'trainer'
    via addressed — nothing else competed for it
lr on Trainer = 2.0
    #0   own                1.0          beaten — earlier in the document
  ✓ #1   bare               2.0          applied
```
**Expected:** the three "bare first" rows give 1.0 on both paths, and ONE applied record — docs/configure.md: "Whichever assignment sits **later in the document wins**, whatever its spelling — there is no 'addressed beats bare'" and "Naming is what lets you apply **the same document `load()` builds from** to objects you already have"; docs/report.md: "`report.applied` — one record per attribute per object — the **final** (last-write-wins) assignment".
**Known?** NEW (CD3/CD5 fixed what the fold copies/drops; the fold's POSITION was never measured against a bare key written above it).
**Same root as:** BC2 (the named-overlay fold in `configure()` carries no position — the broadcast lens measured the load/configure split) — folded into this entry.

## CD2 · S2 — `configure()` re-parses every string value with `parse_value`: a `str` slot cannot take `"123"`/`"yes"`/a date-shaped string (raw pydantic error), an untyped slot is silently retyped; `load()` keeps the string
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:297-298` — `if isinstance(value, str): value = parse_value(value)` (applied to every settled string, YAML-quoted ones included; `parse_value` is `yaml.safe_load` plus the true/false/null words — `resolver.py:907-926`).
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure, load

@configurable
class Named:
    def __init__(self, tag: str = "", version: str = "1.0"):
        self.tag, self.version = tag, version

@configurable
class Box:
    def __init__(self, a=None):
        self.a = a

for value in ("123", "yes", "2026-08-22", "1:30", "0x10"):
    b = Box()
    configure(b, config={"a": value})
    print(f"untyped slot: configure(a={value!r}) -> {b.a!r}")
print("untyped slot: load(a: '123')        ->", repr(load("b: {_target_: Box, a: '123'}")["b"].a))
print("str slot:     load(tag: '123')      ->", repr(load("n: {_target_: Named, tag: '123'}")["n"].tag))
n = Named()
try:
    configure(n, config="tag: '123'\n")
    print("str slot:     configure(tag: '123') ->", repr(n.tag))
except Exception as exc:
    print(f"str slot:     configure(tag: '123') RAISED {type(exc).__module__}.{type(exc).__name__}: {' '.join(str(exc).split())[:110]}")
```
**Actual output:**
```
untyped slot: configure(a='123') -> 123
untyped slot: configure(a='yes') -> True
untyped slot: configure(a='2026-08-22') -> datetime.date(2026, 8, 22)
untyped slot: configure(a='1:30') -> 90
untyped slot: configure(a='0x10') -> 16
untyped slot: load(a: '123')        -> '123'
str slot:     load(tag: '123')      -> '123'
str slot:     configure(tag: '123') RAISED pydantic_core._pydantic_core.ValidationError: 1 validation error for NamedConfig tag Input should be a valid string [type=string_type, input_value=123, inpu
```
**Expected:** a YAML-quoted `'123'` (and a Python `"123"`) stays the string on both paths; a `str`-typed slot accepts it — docs/configure.md: "A document applied to live objects reads exactly as it does at load time"; and the failure, if any, is typed and located — AGENTS "Typed exceptions": "Every error confluid raises MUST come from `confluid/exceptions.py` … never a bare builtin". (`tests/test_configurator.py::test_type_coercion` pins only the int-slot happy path.)
**Known?** NEW.
## CD3 · S2 — A constructor parameter named `include` is consumed as the `include:` directive: `dump()`→`load()` raises (naming a phantom `string.yaml`) or silently splices a file and resets the parameter; `configure()` on such an object dies the same way
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:187-188` — `kwargs = {p: _class_value_spelling(v) for p, v in dumpable_kwargs(data).items()}` / `represent_mapping(…, {TARGET_KEY: cls_name, **kwargs})` writes the slot as a sibling key of `_target_`; `dumper.py:327` does the same for `to_markers` (`marker.kwargs = {k: to_markers(v, memo) …}`); the reader is the loader's include walk, which treats a marker's kwargs as a mapping (`loader.py:975` "Traverse into Class/Fluid kwargs. The kwargs mapping goes through the DICT …", `:1011` `if "include" in processed_dict:`). Neither side escapes or refuses the name.
**Repro:**
```python
import pathlib
from confluid import configurable, configure, load, dump

@configurable
class Source:
    def __init__(self, include: str = "*.wav", lr: float = 0.1):
        self.include, self.lr = include, lr

text = dump(Source(include="*.flac"))
print("dump:", repr(text))
try:
    load(text)
except Exception as exc:
    print(f"load(dump) RAISED {type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:120]}...")

pathlib.Path("extra.yaml").write_text("sneaky: 1\n")          # a value that happens to name a file
back = load(dump(Source(include="extra.yaml")))
print("reload include =", repr(back.include), "| sneaky attr =", getattr(back, "sneaky", "<missing>"))

s = Source(include="*.flac")
try:
    configure(s, config={"lr": 0.2})
except Exception as exc:
    print(f"configure() RAISED {type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:80]}...")
```
**Actual output:**
```
2026-08-22 05:24:06 | WARNING  | confluid.engine:_warn_undeclared:1425 | Source has no attribute 'sneaky' at <unicode string>:1:1 — set as a post-init attribute anyway. Declare it as a constructor parameter or an __init__-body slot, or remove it.
dump: "_target_: Source\ninclude: '*.flac'\nlr: 0.1\n"
load(dump) RAISED ConfigFileNotFoundError: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/scratchpad/hunt/CD/work/repro/string.yam...
reload include = '*.wav' | sneaky attr = 1
configure() RAISED ConfigFileNotFoundError: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-gert-taidal/7b14aa64-a2bc-4de0-847a-9e897af1e1a4/...
```
**Expected:** `load(dump(Source(include="*.flac"))).include == "*.flac"` and `configure(s, …)` applies `lr` — AGENTS "Serialization": "`dump()` followed by `load()` MUST reconstruct an identical object graph. Round-trip fidelity is non-negotiable." Also the error names a file that does not exist (`…/repro/string.yaml`) — AGENTS: "EVERY error raised while processing a document NAMES ITS `file:line:col`".
**Known?** NEW.
## CD4 · S2 — A property WITH a setter: `configure()` writes through the setter, `dump()` emits the stale captured constructor value
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:241-250` — `if isinstance(member, property): … if p in captured … kwargs[p] = captured[p]; continue` — the property branch never reads the live value (the CD2 fix stopped the GETTER running) and `configurator._set` (`:402` `setattr(obj, attr, value)`) never refreshes `__confluid_kwargs__`.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure, load, dump

@configurable
class Model:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):
        self._lr = lr
    @property
    def lr(self) -> float:
        return self._lr
    @lr.setter
    def lr(self, value: float) -> None:
        self._lr = value

m = Model(lr=0.1)
configure(m, config={"lr": 0.5})
print("after configure: m.lr =", m.lr)
text = dump(m)
print("dump:", repr(text))
print("reload lr =", load(text).lr)
```
**Actual output:**
```
after configure: m.lr = 0.5
dump: '_target_: Model\nlr: 0.1\n'
reload lr = 0.1
```
**Expected:** `lr: 0.5` — docs/serialization.md: "the **live attribute of the same name**, when the instance still carries it — so post-construction changes (a `configure()` call, a broadcast) are what you get back".
**Known?** NEW (CD2 fixed the getter half; a settable property is the other half).
## CD5 · S2 — `$$` is not collapsed in the KEYS of a dict nested inside a marker's kwargs: `Box(a={"$k": 1})` reloads as `{"$$k": 1}`
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:334` — `_represent_escaped_str` escapes every `str`, keys included ("uniform over keys and values", `:374-378`); the collapse lives ONLY in `resolver.py:578-582` (the plain-`dict` branch of `resolve`). Marker kwargs go through `_interpolate_fluid_kwargs` → `_interpolate_only`, whose dict branch (`resolver.py:637-640`: `for key in list(value): value[key] = self._interpolate_only(value[key], value, seen)`) rewrites values and never touches keys.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, load, dump

@configurable
class Box:
    def __init__(self, a=None):
        self.a = a

text = dump(Box(a={"$k": 1, "a$b": 2}))
print("dump:", repr(text))
print("reload a =", load(text).a)
print("top-level dict key, same spelling:", load("x: {a$$b: 1}"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
dump: '_target_: Box\na:\n  $$k: 1\n  a$$b: 2\n'
reload a = {'$$k': 1, 'a$$b': 2}
top-level dict key, same spelling: {'x': {'a$b': 1}}
```
**Expected:** `{'$k': 1, 'a$b': 2}` — AGENTS "Serialization": "the escape is uniform over keys and values (a representer cannot tell them apart), and the loader collapses `$$` in mapping keys during the interpolation pass."
**Known?** NEW (CD10's pin `test_a_dollar_in_a_mapping_key_round_trips` covers a TOP-LEVEL key only).
## CD6 · S2 — `dump()` of a `**kwargs` class constructed in Python loses its extras (the load-built twin keeps them)
**Where:** `confluid/decorators.py:476-481` — `cleaned = {name: value … if sig.parameters[name].kind not in (VAR_POSITIONAL, VAR_KEYWORD)}` drops the whole `**kwargs` bundle from `__confluid_kwargs__`; `dumper.py:264-267` then has nothing to emit (`for name, val in captured.items(): …`).
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, load, dump

@configurable
class Wrap:
    def __init__(self, a: int = 1, **kwargs):
        self.a = a
        self.kwargs = dict(kwargs)

w = Wrap(a=2, foo=3)
print("captured:", w.__confluid_kwargs__, "| dump:", repr(dump(w)), "| reload.kwargs:", load(dump(w)).kwargs)
w2 = load("_target_: Wrap\na: 2\nfoo: 3\n")
print("load-built twin — captured:", w2.__confluid_kwargs__, "| dump:", repr(dump(w2)))
```
**Actual output:**
```
captured: {'a': 2} | dump: '_target_: Wrap\na: 2\n' | reload.kwargs: {}
load-built twin — captured: {'a': 2, 'foo': 3} | dump: '_target_: Wrap\na: 2\nfoo: 3\n'
```
**Expected:** `foo: 3` in the dump whichever way the object was built — docs/serialization.md: "A `**kwargs` class's captured extras are emitted too (they *are* constructor arguments)."
**Known?** NEW — CD11 is marked FIXED, but its pin (`test_a_kwargs_classes_captured_extras_survive_the_round_trip`) builds the object with `load()`; the Python-construction capture was never covered.
## CD7 · S2 — `configure()` on a `**kwargs` class, then `dump()`: the configured key is gone (the load-path twin keeps it)
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:402` — `setattr(obj, attr, value)` records nothing on `__confluid_extra__`; `dumper.py:270-275` emits post-init attributes from that list only, and a `**kwargs` class has no declared slot for the key.

```python
w2 = load("w: {_target_: Wrap}\nfoo: 5\n")["w"]

```
**Actual output:**

```
dump: '_target_: Wrap\na: 1\n' | reload foo = <missing>
load-path twin: dump: '_target_: Wrap\na: 1\nfoo: 5\n'
```
**Expected:** `foo: 5` in the dump — docs/serialization.md: "post-construction changes (a `configure()` call, a broadcast) are what you get back".
**Known?** NEW.
## CD8 · S2 — A class-attribute slot (`batch_size = 32`) configured by `configure()` is not dumped; the reload restores the class default (the load-path twin dumps it)
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:30` — `_DUMP_KINDS = frozenset({"keyword", "positional_only", "body_slot"})` excludes `class_attr`, although the accept-list admits it (the bare key lands) and `to_pydantic` types it.
**Repro:**
```python

@configurable
class Node:
    batch_size = 32

        self.lr = lr

n = Node()

print("dump:", repr(dump(n)), "| reload batch_size =", load(dump(n)).batch_size)
n2 = load("n: {_target_: Node}\nbatch_size: 64\n")["n"]
print("load-path twin: dump:", repr(dump(n2)))
```
**Actual output:**
```
after configure: batch_size = 64 | applied: [('batch_size', 'bare')]
dump: '_target_: Node\nlr: 0.1\n' | reload batch_size = 32
load-path twin: dump: '_target_: Node\nlr: 0.1\nbatch_size: 64\n'
```
**Expected:** `batch_size: 64` in the dump — AGENTS "Serialization": "BODY SLOTS are dumped, and every value is dumped ALWAYS … Every OTHER surface treats a body slot as first-class (`configure()` sets it, `to_pydantic` types it, the accept-list admits it), so the omission contradicted the round-trip rule" — the same three surfaces admit a class-attribute slot; docs/configure.md: "A **bare key** lands on every walked object whose accept-list carries it — constructor params, settable properties, and `__init__`-body slots".
**Known?** NEW.
## CD9 · S2 — A tuple value dumps as a list and reloads as a list, even on a `Tuple[float, float]`-annotated slot (`betas=(0.5, 0.6)` → `[0.5, 0.6]`)
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:443` — `yaml.dump(obj, Dumper=_LocalDumper, …)`: `SafeDumper` represents `tuple` with `represent_list`, the dumper registers no tuple spelling, and the reload's validation (`validate_kwargs`) accepts the list without coercing it back.
**Repro:**
```python
from typing import Tuple
from confluid import configurable, load, dump

@configurable
class Opt:
    def __init__(self, betas: Tuple[float, float] = (0.9, 0.999)):
        self.betas = betas

o = Opt(betas=(0.5, 0.6))
text = dump(o)
back = load(text)
print("dump:", repr(text))
print("original:", repr(o.betas), "| reloaded:", repr(back.betas), "| equal:", back.betas == o.betas)
```
**Actual output:**
```
dump: '_target_: Opt\nbetas:\n- 0.5\n- 0.6\n'
original: (0.5, 0.6) | reloaded: [0.5, 0.6] | equal: False
```
**Expected:** an equal graph (`back.betas == o.betas`) — AGENTS "Serialization": "`dump()` followed by `load()` MUST reconstruct an identical object graph"; the workspace mandate on range marks names exactly this slot shape (`Annotated[Tuple[float, float], Interval(...)]`).
**Known?** NEW (ENG-2 covers MARKERS inside a tuple; the plain-tuple type loss was not filed).
## CD10 · S2 — A marker of a DIFFERENT class at a named object's key applies that class's kwargs onto the caller's object (a shared name lands silently, reported `applied`)
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:143` — `deep_merge({k: document[k]}, {k: v})` replaces the object's marker with the `Other` marker; `:166-167` — `if isinstance(node, Target): _apply(objects[name], node, …)` then writes the settled `Other` kwargs onto the `Trainer`. `_tunes_live_child` (`:340-346`) guards SLOTS only; the root-level named key has no class check.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure

@configurable
class Trainer:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.001):
        self.lr = lr

@configurable
class Other:
    def __init__(self, x: int = 0, lr: float = 7.0):
        self.x, self.lr = x, lr

t = Trainer()
report = configure(trainer=t, config="trainer: !class:Other {lr: 5.0}\n")
print("type(t):", type(t).__name__, "| t.lr:", t.lr, "| applied:", [(a.key, a.target, a.origin) for a in report.applied], "| failed:", report.failed)
t = Trainer()
report = configure(trainer=t, config="trainer: !class:Other {x: 1}\n")
print("type(t):", type(t).__name__, "| vars:", {k: v for k, v in vars(t).items() if not k.startswith('__')}, "| failed:", [(f.key, f.reason) for f in report.failed])
```
**Actual output:**
```
2026-08-22 05:24:42 | WARNING  | confluid.engine:_warn_undeclared:1425 | Trainer has no attribute 'x' at <unicode string>:1:10 — set as a post-init attribute anyway. Declare it as a constructor parameter or an __init__-body slot, or remove it.
```

## CD11 · S2 — `configure()` on a marker passed DIRECTLY sets `lr` as an attribute of the `Fluid` object instead of tuning `marker.kwargs`; the report says applied
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:117` — `node = to_markers(obj, memo)` (a `Fluid` passes through as a copy, `dumper.py:300-311`); `:166-167` — `if isinstance(node, Target): _apply(objects[name], node, …)` hands the MARKER to `_apply` as if it were a live object, so `:254` `getattr(obj, attr, _MISSING)` / `:402` `setattr(obj, attr, value)` write on the Fluid. class Opt:

```
applied: [('lr', 'Opt', 'bare')] | unused: []
```

## CD13 · S3 — A `setattr` failure under `configure()` escapes raw and unlocated (validating `__setattr__` → `TypeError`, `__slots__` + named-overlay typo → `AttributeError`, frozen dataclass → `FrozenInstanceError`); the load twin is a located `ConstructionError`
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:402` — `setattr(obj, attr, value)` with no wrap; the load path wraps the same failure at `engine.py:1333-1349` (ENG-10) into `ConstructionError(… cannot accept {k!r} (set at {loc}) …)`.
**Repro:**
```python
import dataclasses
from confluid import configurable, configure, load

@configurable
class Picky:
    def __init__(self, child=None):
        self.child = child
    def __setattr__(self, key, value):
        if key == "child" and isinstance(value, int):
            raise TypeError("cannot assign 'int' as child module (Picky)")
        object.__setattr__(self, key, value)

@configurable
class Slotted:
    __slots__ = ("lr", "__confluid_kwargs__")
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):
        self.lr = lr

@configurable
@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)
class Frozen:
    lr: float = 0.1

for label, call in [
    ("validating __setattr__ (class block)", lambda: configure(Picky(), config="Picky:\n  child: 5\n")),
    ("__slots__ + named-overlay typo      ", lambda: configure(s=Slotted(), config={"s": {"typo": 1}})),
    ("frozen dataclass, bare key          ", lambda: configure(Frozen(), config={"lr": 0.5})),
    ("load twin of the first              ", lambda: load("p: {_target_: Picky}\nPicky: {child: 5}\n")),
]:
    try:
        call(); print(label, "-> no error")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(label, f"-> {type(exc).__module__}.{type(exc).__name__}: {' '.join(str(exc).split())[:100]}")
```
**Actual output:**
```
2026-08-22 05:24:43 | WARNING  | confluid.engine:_warn_undeclared:1425 | Slotted has no attribute 'typo' — set as a post-init attribute anyway. Declare it as a constructor parameter or an __init__-body slot, or remove it.
validating __setattr__ (class block) -> builtins.TypeError: cannot assign 'int' as child module (Picky)
__slots__ + named-overlay typo       -> builtins.AttributeError: 'Slotted' object has no attribute 'typo'
frozen dataclass, bare key           -> dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError: cannot assign to field 'lr'
load twin of the first               -> confluid.exceptions.ConstructionError: Failed to construct Picky at <unicode string>:1:4: cannot assign 'int' as child module (Picky)
```
**Expected:** a `ConfluidError` naming the receiver and the key (and the YAML line when the config was text) on the configure path too — AGENTS: "a validating `__setattr__`'s `TypeError` joins the `AttributeError` wrap at BOTH post-init setattr sites" (ENG-10) and "EVERY error raised while processing a document NAMES ITS `file:line:col`".
**Known?** NEW (ENG-10 fixed the load path only).

## CD15 · S4 — `hydraide.check()` reads its path verbatim: no search tiers, no `~`; `emit()` resolves both — and the failure is a raw `FileNotFoundError`
**Where:** `confluid/hydraide.py:125-126` — `path = Path(path)` / `current = path.read_text()` (no `resolve_config_path`, no `expanduser`); `emit` at `:110` goes through `load()`, which resolves (`loader.py:122-137`). The CLI is unaffected because `cli._resolve_config` resolves first; the documented Python API is not.

```python
except Exception as exc:

home = pathlib.Path.home() / ".cd15_probe.yaml"
home.write_text("x: 1\n")
try:

    print("check('~/.cd15_probe.yaml') ->", repr(check("~/.cd15_probe.yaml")))
except Exception as exc:

finally:

```
**Actual output:**

```
emit('exp.yaml')  -> 'x: 1\n'
check('exp.yaml') RAISED builtins.FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: 'exp.yaml'

check('~/.cd15_probe.yaml') RAISED builtins.FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: '~/.cd15_probe.yaml'
```

## CD16 · S4 — Directive keys consumed by passes 2–4 (`include`, `import`, `default_scopes`, `scope_aliases`) are reported `unused` by `configure()`
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:102-108` — every top-level config key except a `ScopeBlock` is registered as an override candidate (`report.add_config_keys((k,))`); the loader pops the directives before pass 7 can mark them, so they can never be marked used.
**Repro:**

```python
from confluid import configurable, configure
sys.path.insert(0, os.getcwd())

    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.001):
        self.lr = lr

    ("include:",        "include: cd16_base.yaml\n"),
    ("import:",         "import: cd16_mod\nlr: 0.9\n"),
    ("default_scopes:", "default_scopes: [fw=a]\nblk:\n  _scope_: {fw: a}\n  lr: 0.9\n"),
    ("scope_aliases:",  "scope_aliases: {fast: [dbg]}\nblk:\n  _scope_: {dbg: }\n  lr: 0.9\n"),
]:

```
**Actual output:**
```
include:         lr=0.9  unused=['include']
import:          lr=0.9  unused=['import']
default_scopes:  lr=0.9  unused=['default_scopes']
scope_aliases:   lr=0.9  unused=['scope_aliases']
```
**Expected:** `unused=[]` — each directive was consumed (the value it delivered is visible: `lr=0.9`); docs/configure.md: "A consumed scope wrapper is structure, never an 'unused' key" — the same holds for the other structure keys; docs/report.md: "`report.unused` — top-level document keys that matched **nothing** across the whole pass".
**Known?** NEW.

## CD20 · S4 — `configure()` executes a `functools.cached_property` getter when a named overlay names it
**Where:** `confluid/configurator.py:240-254` — `member = getattr(cls, attr, None)`; `if isinstance(member, property): current = _MISSING` misses `cached_property` (not a `property` subclass), so `current = getattr(obj, attr, _MISSING)` runs the descriptor; the named overlay bypasses the scanner's accept-list gate that keeps a class block away from it.

```python
import functools
from confluid import configurable, configure

@configurable
class Lazy:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):

        return 42

lazy = Lazy()
report = configure(lazy=lazy, config={"lazy": {"expensive": 1}})
print("vars(lazy)['expensive'] =", vars(lazy).get("expensive"), "| failed:", [(f.key, f.reason) for f in report.failed])
```

```
2026-08-22 05:25:03 | WARNING  | confluid.engine:_warn_undeclared:1425 | Lazy has no attribute 'expensive' — set as a post-init attribute anyway. Declare it as a constructor parameter or an __init__-body slot, or remove it.
   cached_property getter EXECUTED
vars(lazy)['expensive'] = 1 | failed: [('expensive', 'unknown-attribute')]
```

## CD21 · S4 — A classmethod (bound method) value dumps as the `builtins.method` placeholder and cannot reload; the staticmethod twin round-trips as `${ref:…}`
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:360-361` — representers registered for `types.FunctionType` and `types.BuiltinFunctionType` only; a `types.MethodType` (which carries `__module__`/`__qualname__` like `Batch.collate`) falls to `_represent_opaque` (`:383`).
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, load, dump

class Batch:
    @classmethod
    def collate(cls, items):
        return items
    @staticmethod
    def collate_static(items):
        return items

@configurable
class Loader:
    def __init__(self, collate_fn=None):
        self.collate_fn = collate_fn

for label, fn in (("staticmethod", Batch.collate_static), ("classmethod", Batch.collate)):
    text = dump(Loader(collate_fn=fn))
    try:
        print(f"{label:12} dump={text!r} reload={load(text).collate_fn!r}")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"{label:12} dump={text!r} RELOAD RAISED {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
```
**Actual output:**
```
2026-08-22 05:25:03 | WARNING  | confluid.dumper:_represent_opaque:107 | dump: a live builtins.method has no reconstructible document spelling — emitted as a bare {_target_: builtins.method} placeholder, which reloads DEFAULT-constructed. Register the class (or mark it @configurable) for a faithful round trip.
staticmethod dump='_target_: Loader\ncollate_fn: ${ref:__main__.Batch.collate_static}\n' reload=<function Batch.collate_static at 0x106604040>
classmethod  dump='_target_: Loader\ncollate_fn:\n  _target_: builtins.method\n' RELOAD RAISED UnknownClassError: Cannot resolve class: builtins.method at <unicode string>:3:3
```
**Expected:** `collate_fn: ${ref:__main__.Batch.collate}` — docs/serialization.md: "including a function-valued param (`collate_fn: ${ref:mypkg.collate_records}`)"; the warning's own claim ("reloads DEFAULT-constructed") is also false here — it reloads with an error.
**Known?** NEW.

## CD24 · S4 — docs/serialization.md says a shared instance is "referenced (`${ref:...}`)"; `dump()` emits PyYAML anchors (`&id001` / `*id001`)
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:443` — `yaml.dump(obj, Dumper=_LocalDumper, …)`: sharing is PyYAML's alias machinery, and without `anchor_names` the names are `id001…`; nothing in the dumper emits `${ref:}` for a shared instance. docs/serialization.md, "What a dump contains": "a shared instance reached twice dumps once and is referenced (`${ref:...}`)".
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, dump

@configurable
class Child:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):
        self.lr = lr

@configurable
class Host:
    def __init__(self, child=None):
        self.child = child

c = Child()
print(dump({"a": Host(c), "b": Host(c)}))
```
**Actual output:**
```
a:
  _target_: Host
  child: &id001
    _target_: Child
    lr: 0.1
b:
  _target_: Host
  child: *id001
```
**Expected:** either the documented `${ref:…}` spelling or a doc that says "a YAML anchor" — the anchor form is what `hydraide` documents ("Shared markers are anchors, named after where they live"); the serialization guide contradicts it. Docs ≠ code.
**Known?** NEW.
## CD25 · S4 — A bare key under `configure()` reaches children held in a LIST but not in a TUPLE; `dump()` then turns the tuple into a list
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:312-315` — `to_markers` recurses into `dict` and `list` only ("everything else is a value"), so a tuple of configurables is not part of the object's document; `_discover_and_register` (`:433-435`) DOES walk `(list, tuple)` and the SafeDumper represents the tuple as a list.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure, load, dump

@configurable
class Stage:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.001):
        self.lr = lr

@configurable
class Pipe:
    def __init__(self):
        self.in_tuple = (Stage(), Stage())
        self.in_list = [Stage(), Stage()]

p = Pipe()
report = configure(p, config={"lr": 0.5})
print("tuple children lr:", [s.lr for s in p.in_tuple], "| list children lr:", [s.lr for s in p.in_list], "| unused:", report.unused)
back = load(dump(p))
print("reloaded in_tuple type:", type(back.in_tuple).__name__)
```
**Actual output:**
```
tuple children lr: [0.001, 0.001] | list children lr: [0.5, 0.5] | unused: []
reloaded in_tuple type: list
```
**Expected:** both containers' children get 0.5 — docs/configure.md: "A **bare key** lands on every walked object whose accept-list carries it" and "`*instances` — … each becomes a document node … so a nested configurable child is reached without naming it".
**Known?** NEW.
## CD26 · S4 — A positional-only constructor parameter is dumped as a kwarg the reload cannot pass
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:30` — `_DUMP_KINDS = frozenset({"keyword", "positional_only", "body_slot"})` emits `positional_only` slots by name; the engine's kwargs-only call (`_construct`, `engine.py:1164` `target(*args, **ctor)`) cannot pass them.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, load, dump

@configurable
class Pos:
    def __init__(self, a, /, b=1):
        self.a, self.b = a, b

text = dump(Pos(5, b=2))
print("dump:", repr(text))
try:
    back = load(text); print("reload a =", back.a, "b =", back.b)
except Exception as exc:
    print(f"reload RAISED {type(exc).__name__}: {' '.join(str(exc).split())[:140]}")
```
**Actual output:**
```
dump: '_target_: Pos\na: 5\nb: 2\n'
reload RAISED ConstructionError: Failed to construct Pos at <unicode string>:1:1: 1 validation error for PosConfig a Field required [type=missing, input_value={'b': 2}, inpu
```
**Expected:** either a reloadable document or a loud refusal at dump time — AGENTS "Serialization": "`dump()` followed by `load()` MUST reconstruct an identical object graph".
**Known?** NEW.
## CD27 · S4 — `dump()` of a `**kwargs` class re-emits bare-key attributes as OWN kwargs (the constructor gets them on reload) and drops a `None`-valued one outright
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:270-275` — `for name in getattr(data, "__confluid_extra__", []): … val = getattr(data, name, None); if val is not None: kwargs[name] = val` — the attribute channel is re-spelled as a marker kwarg (which `engine._flow_target` feeds to a `**kwargs` ctor) and a legal `None` is skipped.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, load, dump

@configurable
class Wrap:
    def __init__(self, a: int = 1, **extra):
        self.a = a
        self.extra = extra

w = load("w: {_target_: Wrap, a: 2}\nfoo: null\nbar: 3\n")["w"]
print("loaded: extra =", w.extra, "| foo attr =", getattr(w, "foo", "<missing>"), "| bar attr =", getattr(w, "bar", "<missing>"), "| __confluid_extra__ =", w.__confluid_extra__)
text = dump(w)
back = load(text)
print("dump:", repr(text))
print("reload: extra =", back.extra, "| foo attr =", getattr(back, "foo", "<missing>"), "| bar attr =", getattr(back, "bar", "<missing>"))
```
**Actual output:**
```
loaded: extra = {} | foo attr = None | bar attr = 3 | __confluid_extra__ = ['foo', 'bar']
dump: '_target_: Wrap\na: 2\nbar: 3\n'
reload: extra = {'bar': 3} | foo attr = <missing> | bar attr = <missing>
```
**Expected:** `foo is None` and `bar == 3` as attributes, `extra == {}` — AGENTS: "a BARE cascading key stays a post-init ATTRIBUTE"; docs/configure.md: "A present `null` **sets** `None`"; round-trip rule.
**Known?** overlaps CD17 (open — its `emit` face; this is the `dump()`-of-a-live-object face, plus the `None` drop).
## CD28 · S4 — A `PartialClass` body slot held INSIDE A LIST: `configure()` tunes it, `load()` does not — parity still broken, direction flipped since C5
**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:314-315` — `to_markers` recurses into lists, so `configure()` sees the list-nested marker and pass 7 tunes it; the load path's construction-time reader `engine.py:1466-1470` (`instance_vars = getattr(instance, "__dict__", None)` … `if not isinstance(attr_val, Fluid): continue`) reads DIRECT slots only.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure, load, PartialClass

@configurable
class Opt:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.0):
        self.lr = lr

@configurable
class Host:
    def __init__(self):
        self.opts = [PartialClass(Opt, lr=0.1)]

h = Host()
configure(h, config={"lr": 0.9})
print("configure: h.opts[0].kwargs =", h.opts[0].kwargs)
print("load:      h.opts[0].kwargs =", load("h: !class:Host\nlr: 0.9\n")["h"].opts[0].kwargs)
```
**Actual output:**
```
configure: h.opts[0].kwargs = {'lr': 0.9}
load:      h.opts[0].kwargs = {'lr': 0.1}
```
**Expected:** one answer on both paths — docs/configure.md: "Both apply the same single matching rule".
**Known?** overlaps C5 (BUGS-2026-08-13, open — filed as "tunable by load, untouchable by configure"; the measurement is now the reverse).
## CD29 · S3 — A cyclic object graph still makes `configure()` die with a raw `RecursionError` — now inside the loader's `import:` walk
**Where:** `confluid/loader.py:963` — `_process_imports` recurses into `data.kwargs.values()` with no visited set; `to_markers` (`dumper.py:326`) memoises the cycle into two markers that reference each other, and `configurator.py:159` `load(merged, until="settled")` hands that cyclic marker graph to pass 2.
**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, configure

@configurable
class Parent:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):
        self.lr = lr
        self.child = None

@configurable
class Child:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):
        self.lr = lr
        self.parent = None

p, c = Parent(), Child()
p.child, c.parent = c, p
try:
    configure(p, config={"lr": 0.5}); print("ok", p.lr, c.lr)
except RecursionError as exc:
    print("RecursionError:", exc)
```
**Actual output:**
```
RecursionError: maximum recursion depth exceeded
```
**Expected:** both objects get 0.5 (the memo at `dumper.py:326` exists "so a cycle meets the memo, not the stack"), or a located `ConfluidError` — AGENTS "Typed exceptions".
**Known?** overlaps CD7 (open; the recursion site has moved from the old walker to `loader._process_imports`).

---

### Suspected, not reproduced — CD

- `Enum` / `Path` values on an UNTYPED slot reload as `str` / `str` (`Color.RED` → `'red'`, `Path('/tmp/x')` → `'/tmp/x'`) — documented CD9 design ("each reloads through the constructor that took it"); a typed slot coerces. Not filed.
- A `Target(...)` recipe held in a body slot reloads BUILT (`self.opt = Target(Model, hidden=2)` → `dump` emits `_target_` without `_partial_`, reload constructs a `Model`) — the emission is pinned (`test_a_MARKER_body_slot_survives_dump_and_reload` asserts no `_partial_` for `TargetHost`) and follows the two-modes rule; a documented divergence, not filed.
- Dict-form reserved keys are not markers: `configure(h, config={"Host": {"child": {"_target_": "Trainer"}}})` assigns the raw dict, and `configure(trainer=t, config=yaml.safe_load(emit(x)))` plants a `_target_` ATTRIBUTE on the object (warned as `unknown-attribute`). AGENTS:416 forbids a configurator/dumper branch and docs/configure.md scopes marker parsing to YAML **text**; noted for the loader reviewer (`load(dict_with__target_)` returns the dict), not filed here.
- `scope_aliases: {fast: [fw=b]}` + `scopes=["fast"]` activates nothing on BOTH `configure()` and `load()` — P13 (BUGS-2026-08-13, open, scopes area); parity holds, not refiled.
- A class block REPLACES a dict-valued plain slot (`Box: {a: {y: 3}}` → `{'y': 3}`) while a named overlay / dotted path MERGES it (`box.a: {y: 3}` → `{'x': 1, 'y': 3}`) — each arm is documented (record 15 assign arm vs P1 `deep_merge`) and `load()` agrees per spelling; not filed.
- A dotted line that delivered a MARKER or a dict (`t.opt: !class:Opt(lr=2)`) is re-emitted twice with PyYAML's default anchor (`opt: &id001 …` / `t.opt: *id001`) — idempotent and correct on reload; cosmetic (the name is not "where it lives"), not filed.
- `hydraide emit x | head` exits 1 silently — Click's own EPIPE handling; not a defect.
- `hydraide emit empty.yaml` prints `{}`; `hydraide check empty.yaml` then fails with `+{}` — arguably correct ("a file hydraide wrote passes"); not filed.
- `str` / `int` SUBCLASS values (`class Tag(str)`) take the audible placeholder and reload as `''` / `0` — the documented CD9 placeholder path; not filed. `np.ndarray` likewise takes the placeholder but the reload CRASHES (`ndarray() missing required argument 'shape'`) while the warning text promises "reloads DEFAULT-constructed" — CD9-adjacent wording, not refiled.
- Class-VALUED params (`Box(a=Box)` → `{_target_: Box}` → reloads an INSTANCE; `a=int` → `'builtins.int'` string) — C8 (BUGS-2026-08-13, open); re-seen, not refiled.
- Bound methods of instances as values → placeholder + reload `UnknownClassError` — no importable spelling exists; CD21 covers the classmethod case that does have one.
- A bare dict at a plain-data slot (`a: {y: 3}` written bare) lands nowhere and is reported unused on both paths — consistent.
- `--scope ''` / `--scope a=b=c` are accepted silently by the CLI — already in the 2026-08-19 suspected list; unchanged.
- Two `@configurable class Dup` registrations from two `import:`ed modules: the second REPLACES with a warning and `hydraide emit` exits 0 — registry area, not filed.

---

# I· introspection / schema export / validation
## N1 · S1 — `dump()` emits a framework base's `__init__`-body internals (owner filter missing), the mirror refuses the dumped document, and a `keras.Model` subclass reloads CORRUPTED

**Where:** `confluid/dumper.py:30` — `_DUMP_KINDS = frozenset({"keyword", "positional_only", "body_slot"})` projects `body_slot` with NO owner filter, while the two sibling readers apply one: `confluid/pydantic_export.py:449` — `if not getattr(slot.owner, "__confluid_configurable__", False): continue` and `confluid/schema.py:142` — `if slot.kind == "body_slot" and not getattr(slot.owner, "__confluid_configurable__", False): continue`.

**Repro:**
```python
import os; os.environ.setdefault("KERAS_BACKEND", "torch")
import keras, yaml
from confluid import configurable, dump, load, to_pydantic
from confluid.introspect import slots

@configurable
class Net(keras.Model):
    def __init__(self, width: int = 8):
        super().__init__()
        self.width = width

n = Net(width=4)
doc = dump(n)
print("dump():\n" + doc)
print("to_pydantic fields:", list(to_pydantic(Net).model_fields))
print("foreign-owned body slots:", sorted(s.name for s in slots(Net) if s.kind == "body_slot" and not getattr(s.owner, "__confluid_configurable__", False))[:20])
data = yaml.safe_load(doc); data.pop("_target_")
try:
    to_pydantic(Net).model_validate(data); print("mirror accepts the dumped document")
except Exception as e:
    print("mirror REFUSES the dumped document:", [l.strip() for l in str(e).splitlines() if "Extra" in l][:2], "…", str(e).splitlines()[0])
print("original call_signature_parameters:", list(n.call_signature_parameters))
m = load(doc)
print("reloaded  call_signature_parameters:", list(m.call_signature_parameters), type(m.call_signature_parameters).__name__)
```

**Actual output:**
```
2026-08-22 05:19:44 | WARNING  | confluid.dumper:_represent_opaque:107 | dump: a live keras.src.utils.tracking.TrackedList has no reconstructible document spelling — emitted as a bare {_target_: keras.src.utils.tracking.TrackedList} placeholder, which reloads DEFAULT-constructed. Register the class (or mark it @configurable) for a faithful round trip.
dump():
_target_: Net
width: 4
compiled: false
steps_per_execution: 1
activity_regularizer: null
autocast: true
built: false
call_signature_parameters:
  _target_: keras.src.utils.tracking.TrackedList
supports_jit: true
name: net

to_pydantic fields: ['width']
foreign-owned body slots: ['activity_regularizer', 'autocast', 'built', 'call_signature_parameters', 'compiled', 'loss', 'name', 'predict_function', 'steps_per_execution', 'supports_jit', 'test_function', 'train_function']
mirror REFUSES the dumped document: ['Extra inputs are not permitted [type=extra_forbidden, input_value=False, input_type=bool]', 'Extra inputs are not permitted [type=extra_forbidden, input_value=1, input_type=int]'] … 8 validation errors for NetConfig
original call_signature_parameters: ['args', 'kwargs']
reloaded  call_signature_parameters: [] TrackedList
```
The same shape with a plain non-`@configurable` Python base (`self.training = True; self.compiled = False` in its `__init__`) dumps `compiled: false / training: true` and the mirror refuses both keys (`p45_dump_vs_schema_base_body.py`). A `torch.nn.Module` base happens NOT to trigger it only because torch writes `super().__setattr__('training', True)`, which the AST scan does not see.

**Expected:** the three readers agree on which body slots a class DECLARES — `dump()` emits only slots owned by a `@configurable` class, like the schema and the hierarchy do — and `load(dump(x))` reconstructs `x`. `AGENTS.md` → "a DECLARED-OPTIONS listing filters body slots by `owner`; the accept-list does not … `get_hierarchy` and `to_pydantic` both report only body slots whose `owner` is `@configurable`" — and `docs/serialization.md`: "`dump()` followed by `load()` MUST reconstruct an identical object graph. Round-trip fidelity is non-negotiable." Here 8 keras internals are emitted, `to_pydantic(Net).model_validate(dump)` is refused with `extra_forbidden`, and the reloaded model's `call_signature_parameters` is an EMPTY placeholder `TrackedList` instead of `['args', 'kwargs']`.
**Known?** NEW (AGENTS' own 522-of-859 measurement names the foreign-owned slots; the dump reader was never given the filter)

---
## N2 · S2 — A class attribute and a same-named `__init__`-body assignment: `configure()` sets it, `dump()` DROPS it, `to_pydantic` / `get_hierarchy` omit it — the round trip reloads the old value

**Where:** `confluid/introspect.py:507-527` — the `dir()` class-attr loop claims the name first (`seen.add(name); out.append(Slot(name, "class_attr", …))`), so `:529-533` (`if name in seen: continue`) never reports the body assignment; `class_attr` is outside `dumper._DUMP_KINDS` (`dumper.py:30`), `pydantic_export._FIELD_KINDS` (`pydantic_export.py:64`) and `schema.py:134`'s kind set.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, dump, load, configure, to_pydantic, get_hierarchy
from confluid.broadcast import accepts_key, declares_key
from confluid.introspect import slots

@configurable
class Trainer:
    epochs = 10            # class-level default
    def __init__(self):
        self.epochs = 10   # instance copy, assigned in the body
        self.lr = 0.1

t = Trainer()
configure(t, config={"epochs": 50, "lr": 0.5})
print("after configure:", t.epochs, t.lr)
doc = dump(t)
print("dump:", doc)
t2 = load(doc)
print("reloaded:", t2.epochs, t2.lr)
print("slots:", [(s.name, s.kind) for s in slots(Trainer)])
print("to_pydantic fields:", list(to_pydantic(Trainer).model_fields))
print("get_hierarchy:", get_hierarchy(Trainer))
print("accepts_key epochs:", accepts_key(Trainer, "epochs"), "declares_key:", declares_key(Trainer, "epochs"))

@configurable
class Trainer2:            # the same class WITHOUT the class-level attribute
    def __init__(self):
        self.epochs = 10
        self.lr = 0.1
t = Trainer2()
configure(t, config={"epochs": 50, "lr": 0.5})
doc = dump(t)
print("dump2:", doc)
print("reloaded2:", load(doc).epochs)
print("to_pydantic2 fields:", list(to_pydantic(Trainer2).model_fields))
```

**Actual output:**
```
after configure: 50 0.5
dump: _target_: Trainer
lr: 0.5

reloaded: 10 0.5
slots: [('epochs', 'class_attr'), ('lr', 'body_slot')]
to_pydantic fields: ['lr']
get_hierarchy: {'Trainer.lr': ('Any', 0.1, '')}
accepts_key epochs: True declares_key: True
dump2: _target_: Trainer2
epochs: 50
lr: 0.5

reloaded2: 50
to_pydantic2 fields: ['epochs', 'lr']
```

**Expected:** `self.epochs = 10` in the body is a body slot whether or not a class attribute of the same name exists — `docs/class-design.md`: "the schema surface (`to_pydantic`) enumerates the `__init__` body and surfaces every non-underscore `self.<name> = …` slot declared by a `@configurable` class in the MRO"; `docs/serialization.md`: "`dump()` emits a `_target_:` mapping covering both kinds of slot — constructor parameters and `__init__`-body attributes". Adding one class-level default silently removes the slot from the schema, the hierarchy and the dump while the accept-list still admits it. (Record 13 notes "A class attribute shadowing a body slot now behaves as written — the key … lands"; it lands, and then vanishes from every declared-options reader.)
**Known?** NEW (N8 fixed the `__slots__` member-descriptor face only — `introspect.py:513-514`)

---
## N3 · S2 — A `TypedDict` / `@dataclass` / pydantic `BaseModel` constructor parameter is made OPAQUE (`{}` schema) although pydantic schemas all three natively

**Where:** `confluid/pydantic_export.py:152` — `TypeAdapter(anno, config=ConfigDict(arbitrary_types_allowed=True)).json_schema()`: pydantic raises `PydanticUserError` ("Cannot use `config` when the type is a BaseModel, dataclass or TypedDict") for exactly those three kinds; `:155-156` — `except Exception: return False` turns that into "not schemable", and `:243-249` wraps the type in `Annotated[T, WithJsonSchema({})]`.

**Repro:**
```python
from typing import TypedDict, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pydantic import BaseModel, TypeAdapter
from confluid import configurable, to_pydantic
from confluid.pydantic_export import _json_schemable, _convert_annotation

class Window(TypedDict):
    start: int
    stop: int
@dataclass
class Crop:
    width: int = 8
    height: int = 8
class Opts(BaseModel):
    lr: float = 0.1

for t in (Window, Crop, Opts):
    print(t.__name__, "| pydantic native schema:", TypeAdapter(t).json_schema())
    print(t.__name__, "| confluid _json_schemable:", _json_schemable(t), "| converted:", _convert_annotation(t))

@configurable
class Host:
    def __init__(self, win: Optional[Window] = None, crop: Crop = Crop(), opts: Optional[Opts] = None):
        self.win = win; self.crop = crop; self.opts = opts

sch = to_pydantic(Host).model_json_schema()
for k in ("win", "crop", "opts"):
    print("Host schema", k, "->", sch["properties"][k])
```

**Actual output:**
```
Window | pydantic native schema: {'properties': {'start': {'title': 'Start', 'type': 'integer'}, 'stop': {'title': 'Stop', 'type': 'integer'}}, 'required': ['start', 'stop'], 'title': 'Window', 'type': 'object'}
Window | confluid _json_schemable: False | converted: typing.Annotated[__main__.Window, WithJsonSchema(json_schema={}, mode=None)]
Crop | pydantic native schema: {'properties': {'width': {'default': 8, 'title': 'Width', 'type': 'integer'}, 'height': {'default': 8, 'title': 'Height', 'type': 'integer'}}, 'title': 'Crop', 'type': 'object'}
Crop | confluid _json_schemable: False | converted: typing.Annotated[__main__.Crop, WithJsonSchema(json_schema={}, mode=None)]
Opts | pydantic native schema: {'properties': {'lr': {'default': 0.1, 'title': 'Lr', 'type': 'number'}}, 'title': 'Opts', 'type': 'object'}
Opts | confluid _json_schemable: False | converted: typing.Annotated[__main__.Opts, WithJsonSchema(json_schema={}, mode=None)]
Host schema win -> {'anyOf': [{}, {'type': 'null'}], 'default': None}
Host schema crop -> {'default': {'width': 8, 'height': 8}}
Host schema opts -> {'anyOf': [{}, {'type': 'null'}], 'default': None}
```

**Expected:** a TypedDict / dataclass / BaseModel field keeps its object schema (properties, required, defaults) — they are the most structured annotations a class can write. `docs/schema-export.md`: "Any other plain class pydantic **cannot** JSON-schema … gets an opaque `{}` schema" — pydantic CAN schema these; the probe's `config=` argument is what fails, not the type. An MCP form / GUI reading `Host`'s schema sees three typeless blobs.
**Known?** NEW (N10's fix introduced the probe; this is its false negative)

---
## N4 · S2 — A `@configurable @dataclass` with `field(default_factory=…)` publishes dataclasses' private `_HAS_DEFAULT_FACTORY` sentinel as the slot's default: the mirror instance holds `<factory>`, `get_hierarchy` reports `<factory>`, every schema read warns

**Where:** `confluid/introspect.py:476` — `default=param.default` (the generated `__init__`'s default IS the sentinel) → `confluid/pydantic_export.py:420` — `return converted_type, Field(default=default, **desc_kw)`; `confluid/schema.py:148` — `default = None if slot.default is NO_DEFAULT else slot.default`.

**Repro:**
```python
# --- file: dc_mod.py ---
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Optional
from confluid import configurable

@configurable
@dataclass
class DC:
    x: int = 1
    names: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    child: Optional[Leaf] = None

@configurable
@dataclass
class Leaf:
    v: float = 0.5

# --- file: p27_dataclass_factory.py ---
import warnings
from dc_mod import DC
from confluid import to_pydantic, input_specs, get_hierarchy
from confluid.introspect import slots
M = to_pydantic(DC)
print("slots default for names:", [s.default for s in slots(DC) if s.name == "names"])
print("model default for names:", M.model_fields["names"].default, "| is_required:", M.model_fields["names"].is_required())
m = M.model_validate({"x": 3})
print("mirror instance names =", repr(m.names))
print("mirror model_dump:", m.model_dump())
print("input_specs names:", [s for s in input_specs(DC) if s["name"] == "names"])
print("get_hierarchy names:", get_hierarchy(DC)["DC.names"])
with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as w:
    warnings.simplefilter("always")
    M.model_json_schema(); M.model_json_schema()
print("schema warnings per read:", len(w), [str(x.message)[:80] for x in w][:1])
```

**Actual output:**
```
2026-08-22 05:18:37 | WARNING  | pydantic.main:model_dump:475 | UserWarning: Pydantic serializer warnings:
  PydanticSerializationUnexpectedValue(Expected `list[str]` - serialized value may not be as expected [field_name='names', input_value=<factory>, input_type=_HAS_DEFAULT_FACTORY_CLASS]) (/Users/gert/taidal/.venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/pydantic/main.py:475)
slots default for names: [<factory>]
model default for names: <factory> | is_required: False
mirror instance names = <factory>
mirror model_dump: {'x': 3, 'names': <factory>, 'child': None}
input_specs names: [{'name': 'names', 'type': 'List', 'required': False, 'nullable': False}]
get_hierarchy names: ('List', <factory>, '')
schema warnings per read: 2 ['Default value <factory> is not JSON serializable; excluding default from JSON sc']
```

**Expected:** the mirror's default is the factory's product (`[]`) or the field is simply defaulted without a value — never a private dataclasses sentinel that an MCP layer then emits as `names: <factory>` (`model_dump` above). `docs/schema-export.md`: "Every constructor parameter becomes a field, with its annotation, its default" — the dataclass's default is `list()`, not `_HAS_DEFAULT_FACTORY`. `AGENTS.md` → "Eager classes are first-class" and N14 make `@configurable @dataclass` a supported shape.
**Known?** NEW

## N5 · S2 — `get_hierarchy` reports a `Partial[T]` / `Mandatory[T]` slot — the canonical deferred-slot spelling — as a leaf typed `"Annotated"`, hiding every nested knob

**Where:** `confluid/schema.py:151` — `if hasattr(param_type, "__confluid_configurable__"):` (and `:83` for the callable branch): an `Annotated[Union[T, Fluid], marker]` alias never carries the class mark, so the walker never recurses; `:147` — `type_str = getattr(param_type, "__name__", str(param_type))` renders the alias as `"Annotated"`.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, get_hierarchy, Target, PartialClass
from confluid.partial import Partial
from confluid.mandatory import Mandatory

@configurable
class Optim:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1, momentum: float = 0.9): ...

@configurable
class T1:
    def __init__(self, opt: Optim = None): ...
@configurable
class T2:
    def __init__(self, opt: Partial[Optim] = Target(Optim, lr=0.01)): ...
@configurable
class T3:
    def __init__(self, opt: Mandatory[Optim] = Target(Optim)): ...
@configurable
class T4:
    def __init__(self):
        self.opt: Partial[Optim] = PartialClass(Optim, lr=0.01)
for c in (T1, T2, T3, T4):
    print(c.__name__, get_hierarchy(c))
```

**Actual output:**
```
T1 {'T1.opt.lr': ('float', 0.1, ''), 'T1.opt.momentum': ('float', 0.9, '')}
T2 {'T2.opt': ('Annotated', Target(Optim, {'lr': 0.01}), '')}
T3 {'T3.opt': ('Annotated', Target(Optim, {}), '')}
T4 {'T4.opt': ('Annotated', None, '')}
```

**Expected:** `T2`/`T3`/`T4` list `opt.lr` and `opt.momentum` like `T1` — `docs/class-design.md` rule 4: "give the slot a type annotation in the body (`self.optimizer: Partial[Optimizer] = …`) so the generated schema can type it"; `docs/io-contract.md`: "subscript with the interface the slot flows into. The canonical spellings: `model: Mandatory[nn.Module] = Target(VisionModel)`". Following the documented spelling costs a CLI `--docs` every nested knob and prints the type as `Annotated`.
**Known?** overlaps I11 (same `hasattr` line; I11 filed the `Optional[Child]` face — this is the spelling the docs prescribe)

## N6 · S2 — A class built by `__new__` (a `NamedTuple` config holder, any `__new__`-constructed class) reports `object.__init__`'s `*args/**kwargs`: zero schema fields, accept-EVERYTHING, no validation

**Where:** `confluid/introspect.py:102-104` — `if inspect.isclass(target): return getattr(target, "__init__", None)` resolves to `object.__init__` when the class constructs through `__new__`; `confluid/introspect.py:456` then enumerates its `(*args, **kwargs)`; `confluid/pydantic_export.py:598` skips the probe (`if init is not object.__init__`) and builds an EMPTY model.

**Repro:**
```python
from typing import NamedTuple
from confluid import configurable, to_pydantic
from confluid.introspect import slots
from confluid.broadcast import accepts_key, accepts_any_key, declares_key

@configurable
class Pt(NamedTuple):
    x: int = 0
    y: int = 1

print("slots(Pt):", [(s.name, s.kind) for s in slots(Pt)])
print("to_pydantic(Pt) fields:", list(to_pydantic(Pt).model_fields))
print("accepts_any_key(Pt):", accepts_any_key(Pt), "accepts_key(Pt,'zzz'):", accepts_key(Pt, "zzz"), "declares_key(Pt,'x'):", declares_key(Pt, "x"))
print("Pt(x='bad'):", Pt(x="bad"))

@configurable
class Frozen:
    def __new__(cls, x: int = 1):
        self = super().__new__(cls)
        self.x = x
        return self

print("slots(Frozen):", [(s.name, s.kind) for s in slots(Frozen)])
print("to_pydantic(Frozen) fields:", list(to_pydantic(Frozen).model_fields))
print("accepts_any_key(Frozen):", accepts_any_key(Frozen))
print("Frozen(x='bad').x =", Frozen(x="bad").x)
```

**Actual output:**
```
slots(Pt): [('args', 'var_positional'), ('kwargs', 'var_keyword'), ('x', 'class_attr'), ('y', 'class_attr')]
to_pydantic(Pt) fields: []
accepts_any_key(Pt): True accepts_key(Pt,'zzz'): True declares_key(Pt,'x'): True
Pt(x='bad'): Pt(x='bad', y=1)
slots(Frozen): [('args', 'var_positional'), ('kwargs', 'var_keyword')]
to_pydantic(Frozen) fields: []
accepts_any_key(Frozen): True
Frozen(x='bad').x = bad
```

**Expected:** the class's real constructor signature (`inspect.signature(Pt)` → `(x: int = 0, y: int = 1)`) governs every reader — `docs/validation.md`: "Every `@configurable` class has its `__init__` wrapped … to validate kwargs against the auto-generated pydantic schema"; `AGENTS.md`: "'Whose signature governs calling this target?' has ONE answer". A class with two typed, defaulted parameters produces an empty model and accepts any bare key.
**Known?** overlaps I3 (I3 is the no-`__init__` face; here the class DOES declare a constructor and it is ignored)

## N7 · S3 — A parameter literally named `cls` is dropped from `slots()`: a `@configurable` builder function refuses the legal call `build(cls=…)`, and `@classmethod` + `@configurable` (the documented order) can never be called

**Where:** `confluid/introspect.py:460-461` — `if name in ("self", "cls"): continue` — a NAME filter applied to every target, including plain functions and `__init__` parameters that are not the receiver; `confluid/decorators.py:201-205` documents the `@classmethod` ABOVE `@configurable` order, whose bound call then fails at `:404` (`validate_kwargs(func, cleaned, mode)`) because `cleaned` carries `cls` and the model has no such field.

**Repro:**
```python
from confluid import configurable, get_hierarchy, to_pydantic, dump
from confluid.broadcast import accepts_key, declares_key
from confluid.introspect import slots

@configurable
def build(cls: type = int, n: int = 1):
    return (cls, n)

print("slots(build):", [(s.name, s.kind) for s in slots(build)])
print("to_pydantic(build) fields:", list(to_pydantic(build).model_fields))
print("accepts_key(build,'cls'):", accepts_key(build, "cls"), "declares_key:", declares_key(build, "cls"))
print("get_hierarchy(build):", get_hierarchy(build))
try:
    print("build(cls=str, n=2) ->", build(cls=str, n=2))
except Exception as e:
    print("build(cls=str, n=2) RAISED", type(e).__name__, str(e).splitlines()[0:3])

@configurable
class Wrap:
    def __init__(self, cls: type = int, n: int = 1):
        self.cls = cls
        self.n = n
print("slots(Wrap):", [(s.name, s.kind) for s in slots(Wrap)])

class Model:
    @classmethod
    @configurable(name="from_pretrained")
    def from_pretrained(cls, name: str = "x"):
        return (cls.__name__, name)
try:
    print("Model.from_pretrained('y') ->", Model.from_pretrained("y"))
except Exception as e:
    print("Model.from_pretrained('y') RAISED", type(e).__name__, str(e).splitlines()[0:3])
```

**Actual output:**
```
slots(build): [('n', 'keyword')]
to_pydantic(build) fields: ['n']
accepts_key(build,'cls'): False declares_key: False
get_hierarchy(build): {'n': ('int', 1, '')}
build(cls=str, n=2) RAISED ValidationError ['1 validation error for buildConfig', 'cls', "  Extra inputs are not permitted [type=extra_forbidden, input_value=<class 'str'>, input_type=type]"]
slots(Wrap): [('n', 'keyword'), ('cls', 'body_slot')]
Model.from_pretrained('y') RAISED ValidationError ['1 validation error for from_pretrainedConfig', 'cls', "  Extra inputs are not permitted [type=extra_forbidden, input_value=<class '__main__.Model'>, input_type=type]"]
```

**Expected:** `AGENTS.md`: "Only a KIND may exclude a slot — a NAME never does (2026-08-13). `to_pydantic`'s `_SKIP_PARAMS` name-set dropped an ordinary parameter literally named `args`/`kwargs` from the model alone among the readers, and `extra="forbid"` then refused the legal constructor call." The same defect survives for the name `cls`: a builder taking `cls` is uncallable, `Wrap.__init__`'s `cls` PARAMETER is misreported as an untyped body slot, and the documented `@classmethod`/`@configurable` order raises on every call.
**Known?** NEW

## N8 · S3 — A parameter typed with a PARAMETERIZED `@configurable` generic (`Box[int]`) gets no nested mirror: `model_json_schema()` raises and the mirror form is refused — the bare `Box` spelling works

**Where:** `confluid/pydantic_export.py:216-227` — the nested-mirror branch (`if _is_configurable(anno): … return Union[anno, nested]`) sits under `if origin is None:`; a subscripted generic has `origin = Box`, falls through `:279` (`_is_opaque_type(origin)` — torch/numpy only) and is rebuilt verbatim at `:297-301`, so pydantic keeps an `IsInstanceSchema`.

**Repro:**
```python
from typing import Generic, TypeVar, Optional
from confluid import configurable, to_pydantic
T = TypeVar("T")

@configurable
class Box(Generic[T]):
    def __init__(self, item: Optional[T] = None):
        self.item = item

@configurable
class Host:
    def __init__(self, box: Optional[Box[int]] = None):
        self.box = box

@configurable
class HostBare:
    def __init__(self, box: Optional[Box] = None):
        self.box = box

for cls in (HostBare, Host):
    M = to_pydantic(cls)
    print(cls.__name__, "field annotation:", M.model_fields["box"].annotation)
    try:
        print(cls.__name__, "schema box:", M.model_json_schema()["properties"]["box"])
    except Exception as e:
        print(cls.__name__, "model_json_schema RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", str(e).splitlines()[0][:160])
    try:
        print(cls.__name__, "mirror-form accepted:", M.model_validate({"box": {"item": 1}}))
    except Exception as e:
        print(cls.__name__, "mirror-form RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", str(e).splitlines()[1:3])
```

**Actual output:**
```
HostBare field annotation: typing.Union[__main__.Box, confluid.pydantic_export.BoxConfig, NoneType]
HostBare schema box: {'anyOf': [{'$ref': '#/$defs/BoxConfig'}, {'type': 'null'}], 'default': None, 'title': 'Box'}
HostBare mirror-form accepted: box=BoxConfig(item=1)
Host field annotation: typing.Optional[__main__.Box[int]]
Host model_json_schema RAISED PydanticInvalidForJsonSchema : Cannot generate a JsonSchema for core_schema.IsInstanceSchema (<class '__main__.Box'>)
Host mirror-form RAISED ValidationError : ['box', "  Input should be an instance of Box [type=is_instance_of, input_value={'item': 1}, input_type=dict]"]
```

**Expected:** `Box[int]` is converted like `Box` — `docs/schema-export.md`: "The generated models mirror each class's `__init__` signature as typed pydantic fields and recursively wrap nested `@configurable` parameter types in their own generated models"; `AGENTS.md` → Schema export: "the mirror is built for EVERY legal signature, and it JSON-schemas". Typing the slot precisely (the `Any`-only-when-needed mandate) makes the schema crash.
**Known?** NEW

## N9 · S3 — A PARAMETERIZED generic whose ORIGIN pydantic cannot JSON-schema (`IO[str]`, `ContextManager[T]`, `Awaitable[T]`) crashes `model_json_schema()` — the bare-leaf rule is not applied to the origin

**Where:** `confluid/pydantic_export.py:243-249` — the `_json_schemable` probe runs only in the `origin is None` branch; `:279-280` — `if _is_opaque_type(origin): return Any` consults the torch/numpy allow-list only, so `IO[str]` is rebuilt at `:297-301` with an `IsInstanceSchema` origin.

**Repro:**
```python
from typing import Any, Optional, IO, ContextManager, Awaitable
from confluid import configurable, to_pydantic

@configurable
class Sink:
    def __init__(self, stream: Optional[IO[str]] = None): self.stream = stream
@configurable
class Ctx:
    def __init__(self, cm: Optional[ContextManager[Any]] = None): self.cm = cm
@configurable
class Aw:
    def __init__(self, fut: Optional[Awaitable[int]] = None): self.fut = fut

for cls in (Sink, Ctx, Aw):
    cls()
    try:
        print(cls.__name__, "schema:", to_pydantic(cls).model_json_schema()["properties"])
    except Exception as e:
        print(cls.__name__, "model_json_schema RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", str(e).splitlines()[0][:120])
```

**Actual output:**
```
Sink model_json_schema RAISED PydanticInvalidForJsonSchema : Cannot generate a JsonSchema for core_schema.IsInstanceSchema (<class 'typing.IO'>)
Ctx model_json_schema RAISED PydanticInvalidForJsonSchema : Cannot generate a JsonSchema for core_schema.IsInstanceSchema (<class 'contextlib.AbstractContextManager'>)
Aw model_json_schema RAISED PydanticInvalidForJsonSchema : Cannot generate a JsonSchema for core_schema.IsInstanceSchema (<class 'collections.abc.Awaitable'>)
```
(Every constructor call succeeded. In the type-zoo probe `p18_type_zoo.py` the BARE `Optional[io.TextIOBase]`, `Optional[queue.Queue]`, `Optional[slice]` spellings rendered `{'anyOf': [{}, {'type': 'null'}], …}` — the N10 fix works for leaves only.)

**Expected:** `AGENTS.md` → Schema export: "A param typed with an un-JSON-schemable leaf (`torch.Tensor`, numpy arrays) — or a PARAMETERIZED generic whose ORIGIN is one — is coerced to `Any` so schema generation never crashes." `docs/schema-export.md`: "Un-schemable leaf types never crash schema generation."
**Known?** NEW (N10 is FIXED for bare leaves; its own note listed `IO[str]` and `Awaitable` as crashing, and they still do in the parameterized form)

## N10 · S3 — A range mark on a `Sequence[float]` (or `Dict[str, float]`) container raises a raw `TypeError` from the constructor on a LEGAL value, and the schema carries an invalid `ge` keyword at the array level

**Where:** `confluid/pydantic_export.py:78` — `_RANGE_CONTAINER_ORIGINS: Set[Any] = {tuple, list, set, frozenset}` and `:335-336` — `if get_origin(inner) not in _RANGE_CONTAINER_ORIGINS: return inner, metadata` — `collections.abc.Sequence` (which `broadcast._classify_annotation:512` treats as a list container and the abstract-collection mandate keeps typed) is not in the set, so the mark stays on the container value.

**Repro:**
```python
from typing import Annotated, Sequence, Dict
from annotated_types import Interval
from confluid import configurable, to_pydantic

@configurable
class RSeq:
    def __init__(self, seq: Annotated[Sequence[float], Interval(ge=0.0)] = (0.0, 1.0)):
        self.seq = seq

@configurable
class RDict:
    def __init__(self, d: Annotated[Dict[str, float], Interval(ge=0.0)] = None):
        self.d = d

for cls, kw in ((RSeq, {"seq": [0.5, 2.0]}), (RDict, {"d": {"a": 1.0}})):
    try:
        cls(**kw); print(cls.__name__, kw, "-> ok")
    except Exception as e:
        print(cls.__name__, kw, "RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", str(e).splitlines()[0][:150])
    print("   schema:", to_pydantic(cls).model_json_schema()["properties"])
```

**Actual output:**
```
RSeq {'seq': [0.5, 2.0]} RAISED TypeError : Unable to apply constraint 'ge' to supplied value [0.5, 2.0]
   schema: {'seq': {'default': [0.0, 1.0], 'ge': 0.0, 'items': {'type': 'number'}, 'title': 'Seq', 'type': 'array'}}
RDict {'d': {'a': 1.0}} RAISED TypeError : Unable to apply constraint 'ge' to supplied value {'a': 1.0}
   schema: {'d': {'additionalProperties': {'type': 'number'}, 'default': None, 'ge': 0.0, 'title': 'D', 'type': 'object'}}
```

**Expected:** the mark relocates onto the numeric elements exactly as for `Tuple`/`List` — `docs/schema-export.md`: "A range mark on a `(min, max)` tuple container is applied per element"; `AGENTS.md` → "`_spread_range_marks_into_container` relocates them onto the container's numeric elements"; and a legal value must never raise a RAW `TypeError` from a `@configurable` constructor (N9's exact failure shape, fixed for `Optional[Tuple]` only). `Sequence[float]` is the spelling the abstract-collection rule tells authors to keep.
**Known?** NEW

## N11 · S3 — An `Ellipsis` default (`x: Any = ...`) makes the mirror field REQUIRED: the legal call `S(y=2)` is refused, while `S()` passes only because zero-kwarg calls are never validated

**Where:** `confluid/pydantic_export.py:420` — `return converted_type, Field(default=default, **desc_kw)` with `default is Ellipsis`, which pydantic reads as "required"; `confluid/validation.py:273-276` — `if not concrete: return` is why `S()` slips through.

**Repro:**
```python
from typing import Any
from confluid import configurable, to_pydantic

@configurable
class S:
    def __init__(self, x: Any = ..., y: int = 1):
        self.x = x
        self.y = y

print("fields:", {k: (v.is_required(), v.default) for k, v in to_pydantic(S).model_fields.items()})
for kw in ({}, {"y": 2}):
    try:
        s = S(**kw); print(f"S(**{kw}) ok:", s.x, s.y)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"S(**{kw}) RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", str(e).splitlines()[0:3])
```

**Actual output:**
```
fields: {'x': (True, PydanticUndefined), 'y': (False, 1)}
S(**{}) ok: Ellipsis 1
S(**{'y': 2}) RAISED ValidationError : ['1 validation error for SConfig', 'x', "  Field required [type=missing, input_value={'y': 2}, input_type=dict]"]
```

**Expected:** a parameter WITH a default is an optional field whatever the default object is — `docs/schema-export.md`: "Every constructor parameter becomes a field, with its annotation, its default"; `docs/validation.md`: the wrap validates "kwargs against the auto-generated pydantic schema" — a legal constructor call must not be refused. (`Ellipsis` as a sentinel default is the idiom pydantic itself and several typing-heavy libraries use.)
**Known?** NEW

## N12 · S4 — `to_pydantic` raises and `get_hierarchy` answers `{}` for a registered `functools.partial` / callable-instance target that `slots()` / `input_specs` / `accepts_key` introspect fine

**Where:** `confluid/pydantic_export.py:605-609` — `get_type_hints(cls, include_extras=True)` raises `TypeError` for a partial / instance ("is not a module, class, method, or function") → `IntrospectionError`; `confluid/schema.py:68` — `if inspect.isroutine(obj):` is False for a partial, so the class path at `:91` reads `functools.partial.__init__`.

**Repro:**
```python
import functools
from confluid import register, to_pydantic, input_specs, get_hierarchy
from confluid.introspect import slots
from confluid.broadcast import accepts_key

def build(width: int = 8, depth: int = 2):
    return (width, depth)

p = functools.partial(build, depth=3)
register(p, name="narrow")
print("slots(partial):", [(s.name, s.kind, s.annotation) for s in slots(p)])
print("input_specs(partial):", input_specs(p))
print("get_hierarchy(partial):", get_hierarchy(p))
print("accepts_key(partial,'width'):", accepts_key(p, "width"))
try:
    print("to_pydantic(partial):", list(to_pydantic(p).model_fields))
except Exception as e:
    print("to_pydantic(partial) RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", e)

class Builder:
    def __call__(self, n: int = 1):
        return n
b = Builder()
register(b, name="b")
print("slots(instance):", [(s.name, s.kind, s.annotation) for s in slots(b)])
try:
    print("to_pydantic(instance):", list(to_pydantic(b).model_fields))
except Exception as e:
    print("to_pydantic(instance) RAISED", type(e).__name__, ":", e)
```

**Actual output:**
```
slots(partial): [('width', 'keyword', <class 'int'>), ('depth', 'keyword', <class 'int'>)]
input_specs(partial): [{'name': 'width', 'type': 'int', 'required': False, 'nullable': False}, {'name': 'depth', 'type': 'int', 'required': False, 'nullable': False}]
get_hierarchy(partial): {}
accepts_key(partial,'width'): True
to_pydantic(partial) RAISED IntrospectionError : Cannot introspect callable functools.partial(<function build at 0x102744cc0>, depth=3): functools.partial(<function build at 0x102744cc0>, depth=3) is not a module, class, method, or function.
slots(instance): [('n', 'keyword', <class 'int'>)]
to_pydantic(instance) RAISED IntrospectionError : Cannot introspect callable <__main__.Builder object at 0x108b9d460>: <__main__.Builder object at 0x108b9d460> is not a module, class, method, or function.
```

**Expected:** one answer per target — `AGENTS.md` → "Registration REFUSES what it cannot name … A provided `name=` always suffices for a nameless callable — a named partial registers and flows (pinned con)" and "Every signature reader goes through `introspect.init_callable`"; `docs/schema-export.md`: "`to_pydantic` accepts a plain builder function as well as a class — the function's own signature is introspected, which is what lets a registered factory surface in a picker exactly like a class does." A picker enumerating registered targets gets a raise for the partial and an empty hierarchy, while the I/O contract and accept-list for the SAME target are correct.
**Known?** NEW (the 08-19 suspected list mentions partials only for `partial_param_names`)

## N13 · S4 — Validation errors on the `configure()` / `configure_from_file()` path — strict AND warn — name neither the file nor the key's line, while `load()` of the same value names `cfg2.yaml:1:4`

**Where:** `confluid/validation.py:363-367` — `except ValidationError as exc: if mode == "strict": raise` (the raw pydantic error, unwrapped) and `logger.warning(f"{cls.__name__}.{name}: invalid value\n{exc}")` — no location, unlike `:300`'s `{_construction_where.get()}` on the load path; the caller `confluid/configurator.py:396` — `detail = validate_setattr(obj.__class__, attr, value, get_policy().init)` — passes no file/node although `configure_from_file(…, path=…)` holds the file.

**Repro:**
```python
from pathlib import Path
from confluid import configurable, configure_from_file, set_policy, load

@configurable
class Opt:
    def __init__(self, lr: float = 0.1):
        self.lr = lr

Path("cfg.yaml").write_text("Opt:\n  lr: not-a-float\n")

o = Opt()
try:
    configure_from_file(o, path="cfg.yaml")
except Exception as e:
    print("RAISED", type(e).__name__, "| message:", str(e).splitlines()[:3])
print("--- strict, load() of the same value ---")
Path("cfg2.yaml").write_text("o: !class:Opt {lr: not-a-float}\n")
try:
    load("cfg2.yaml")
except Exception as e:
    print("RAISED", type(e).__name__, "|", str(e).splitlines()[0][:120])
print("--- warn, configure_from_file ---")
set_policy(init="warn")
o = Opt()
configure_from_file(o, path="cfg.yaml")
print("lr after:", o.lr)
```
